--- START OF FILE: .\src\cli.py ---
"""ResistAI CLI -- AMR drug discovery powered by NVIDIA NIM biology models.

Usage: python -m src.cli <command> [options]
       resistai <command> [options]
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import sys
import time
from dataclasses import asdict
from pathlib import Path

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ANSI helpers (no deps)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
_NO_COLOR = os.environ.get("NO_COLOR") or not sys.stdout.isatty()

def _c(code: str, text: str) -> str:
    return text if _NO_COLOR else f"\033[{code}m{text}\033[0m"

def bold(t: str) -> str:   return _c("1", t)
def dim(t: str) -> str:    return _c("2", t)
def red(t: str) -> str:    return _c("31", t)
def green(t: str) -> str:  return _c("32", t)
def yellow(t: str) -> str: return _c("33", t)
def blue(t: str) -> str:   return _c("34", t)
def cyan(t: str) -> str:   return _c("36", t)
def mag(t: str) -> str:    return _c("35", t)

def badge(label: str, ok: bool) -> str:
    sym = green("ON") if ok else red("OFF")
    return f"[{label}: {sym}]"

def table(headers: list[str], rows: list[list[str]], widths: list[int] | None = None) -> str:
    """Render a simple ASCII table."""
    if not widths:
        widths = [max(len(h), *(len(str(r[i])) for r in rows)) + 2 for i, h in enumerate(headers)]
    sep = "+" + "+".join("-" * w for w in widths) + "+"
    def _row(vals: list[str]) -> str:
        cells = [str(v).ljust(w) for v, w in zip(vals, widths)]
        return "|" + "|".join(cells) + "|"
    lines = [sep, _row(headers), sep]
    for r in rows:
        lines.append(_row(r))
    lines.append(sep)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def progress(msg: str, pct: float) -> None:
    bar_w = 30
    filled = int(bar_w * pct)
    bar = green("=" * filled) + dim("-" * (bar_w - filled))
    sys.stderr.write(f"\r  [{bar}] {pct*100:5.1f}%  {msg[:60]:<60}")
    sys.stderr.flush()
    if pct >= 1.0:
        sys.stderr.write("\n")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Env loading
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _load_env() -> None:
    """Load .env from project root into os.environ (simple key=value parser)."""
    for candidate in [Path.cwd() / ".env", Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / ".env"]:
        if candidate.is_file():
            with open(candidate) as f:
                for line in f:
                    line = line.strip()
                    if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
                        continue
                    k, v = line.split("=", 1)
                    v = v.strip().strip("'\"")
                    os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v)
            break

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Lazy imports (keep startup fast)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _nim():
    from src.models.nim_client import NIMClient
    return NIMClient()

def _orchestrator(cb=None):
    from src.pipeline.orchestrator import PipelineOrchestrator
    return PipelineOrchestrator(progress_callback=cb)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Command handlers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def cmd_run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Run the full AMR drug discovery pipeline."""
    _load_env()
    print(bold(cyan("\n  ResistAI  ")) + dim("AMR Drug Discovery Pipeline"))
    print(dim(f"  Pathogen: {args.pathogen}  |  Max drugs: {args.max_drugs}  |  Novel: {args.novel}\n"))

    def _cb(stage: str, pct: float, msg: str) -> None:
        if not args.json:
            progress(msg, pct)

    t0 = time.time()
    orch = _orchestrator(cb=_cb)
    try:
        results = orch.run_full_pipeline(
            pathogen_name=args.pathogen,
            max_drugs=args.max_drugs,
            generate_novel=args.novel,
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        print(red(f"\n  Error: {exc}"))
        return 1

    dur = time.time() - t0

    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps(results.to_dict(), indent=2))
        return 0

    # Pretty output
    meta = results.metadata
    models = meta.get("models_used", [])
    print(bold("\n  Models used: ") + "  ".join(badge(m.split("(")[0].strip(), True) for m in models))
    print()

    # Targets table
    print(bold("  Targets Discovered"))
    rows = [[t.name, t.pdb_code, f"{t.druggability_score:.3f}", t.function[:40]] for t in results.targets]
    print(table([" Name", " PDB", " Score", " Function"], rows, [18, 8, 9, 44]))

    # Top candidates table
    if results.top_candidates:
        print(bold("\n  Top Drug Candidates"))
        rows = []
        for c in results.top_candidates[:10]:
            rows.append([
                f" #{c.rank}", f" {c.drug_name}", f" {c.binding_affinity:.3f}",
                f" {c.safety_score:.3f}", f" {c.overall_score:.4f}", f" {c.regulatory_pathway}",
            ])
        print(table([" Rank", " Drug", " Binding", " Safety", " Overall", " Pathway"],
                     rows, [7, 24, 10, 9, 10, 14]))

    # Novel molecules
    novels = meta.get("novel_molecules", [])
    if novels:
        print(bold(f"\n  Novel Molecules Generated: {len(novels)}"))
        for n in novels[:5]:
            smi = n.get("smiles", "?")[:50]
            print(f"    {green('*')} QED={n.get('qed_score', 0):.3f}  seed={n.get('seed_drug', '?')}  {dim(smi)}")

    print(dim(f"\n  Completed in {dur:.1f}s  |  {meta.get('compounds_screened', 0)} compounds screened\n"))
    return 0


def cmd_predict(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Predict protein-ligand binding via Boltz-2."""
    _load_env()
    nim = _nim()
    print(bold(cyan("  Boltz-2 Binding Prediction\n")) if not args.json else "", end="")
    t0 = time.time()
    result = nim.predict_binding(args.sequence, args.smiles)
    dur = time.time() - t0

    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps(asdict(result) | {"duration_s": round(dur, 2)}, indent=2, default=str))
        return 0 if result.success else 1

    if not result.success:
        print(red("  Prediction failed. Check API key and inputs."))
        return 1

    rows = [
        [" Confidence", f" {result.confidence_score:.4f}"],
        [" pTM", f" {result.ptm_score:.4f}"],
        [" ipTM", f" {result.iptm_score:.4f}"],
        [" Ligand ipTM", f" {result.ligand_iptm_score:.4f}"],
        [" Complex pLDDT", f" {result.complex_plddt:.4f}"],
        [" Complex ipLDDT", f" {result.complex_iplddt:.4f}"],
    ]
    print(table([" Metric", " Value"], rows, [18, 14]))
    print(dim(f"  {dur:.1f}s elapsed\n"))
    return 0


def cmd_fold(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Fold a protein sequence via ESMFold."""
    _load_env()
    nim = _nim()
    print(bold(cyan("  ESMFold Protein Structure Prediction\n")) if not args.json else "", end="")
    t0 = time.time()
    result = nim.fold_protein(args.sequence)
    dur = time.time() - t0

    if args.json:
        d = asdict(result)
        d["pdb_string"] = d["pdb_string"][:200] + "..." if len(d["pdb_string"]) > 200 else d["pdb_string"]
        print(json.dumps(d | {"duration_s": round(dur, 2)}, indent=2, default=str))
        return 0 if result.success else 1

    if not result.success:
        print(red("  Folding failed. Check API key and sequence."))
        return 1

    if args.output:
        Path(args.output).write_text(result.pdb_string)
        print(green(f"  Wrote PDB to {args.output}"))
    else:
        print(dim(f"  PDB structure: {len(result.pdb_string)} chars (use --output to save)"))

    print(f"  Sequence length: {result.sequence_length}")
    print(dim(f"  {dur:.1f}s elapsed  |  {result.elapsed_ms:.0f}ms API time\n"))
    return 0


def cmd_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Generate novel molecules via MolMIM."""
    _load_env()
    nim = _nim()
    print(bold(cyan("  MolMIM Molecule Generation\n")) if not args.json else "", end="")
    t0 = time.time()
    result = nim.generate_molecules(
        seed_smiles=args.seed,
        num_molecules=args.n,
        property_name=args.optimize,
        min_similarity=0.3,
    )
    dur = time.time() - t0

    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps(asdict(result) | {"duration_s": round(dur, 2)}, indent=2, default=str))
        return 0 if result.success else 1

    if not result.success:
        print(red("  Generation failed. Check API key and seed SMILES."))
        return 1

    print(f"  Generated {green(str(result.num_generated))} molecules  |  optimize: {args.optimize}")
    rows = []
    for i, mol in enumerate(result.molecules):
        smi = mol.get("sample", "?")
        score = mol.get("score", 0)
        rows.append([f" {i+1}", f" {score:.4f}", f" {smi[:52]}"])
    print(table([" #", f" {args.optimize}", " SMILES"], rows, [5, 10, 55]))
    print(dim(f"  {dur:.1f}s elapsed\n"))
    return 0


def cmd_dock(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Dock a molecule into a protein via DiffDock."""
    _load_env()
    nim = _nim()
    print(bold(cyan("  DiffDock Molecular Docking\n")) if not args.json else "", end="")

    # Need a folded structure first
    if not args.json:
        print(dim("  Folding protein with ESMFold first..."))
    fold = nim.fold_protein(args.sequence)
    if not fold.success:
        print(red("  Could not fold protein. Cannot dock."))
        return 1

    t0 = time.time()
    result = nim.dock_molecule(fold.pdb_string, args.smiles, num_poses=args.poses)
    dur = time.time() - t0

    if args.json:
        d = asdict(result)
        d["ligand_positions"] = [p[:80] + "..." if len(p) > 80 else p for p in d["ligand_positions"]]
        print(json.dumps(d | {"duration_s": round(dur, 2)}, indent=2, default=str))
        return 0 if result.success else 1

    if not result.success:
        print(red("  Docking failed."))
        return 1

    print(f"  Generated {green(str(result.num_poses))} docking poses")
    rows = []
    for i, conf in enumerate(result.position_confidence):
        rows.append([f" {i+1}", f" {conf:.4f}"])
    print(table([" Pose", " Confidence"], rows, [8, 14]))
    print(dim(f"  {dur:.1f}s elapsed\n"))
    return 0


def cmd_design_binder(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Design a protein binder via RFdiffusion + ProteinMPNN."""
    _load_env()
    nim = _nim()
    print(bold(cyan("  RFdiffusion + ProteinMPNN Binder Design\n")) if not args.json else "", end="")

    if not args.json:
        print(dim("  Folding target protein..."))
    fold = nim.fold_protein(args.sequence)
    if not fold.success:
        print(red("  Could not fold target protein."))
        return 1

    t0 = time.time()
    rf = nim.design_binder(fold.pdb_string, contigs=args.contigs)
    if not rf.success:
        print(red("  RFdiffusion design failed."))
        return 1 if not args.json else (print(json.dumps({"success": False})) or 1)

    mpnn = nim.design_sequence(rf.pdb_string)
    dur = time.time() - t0

    out = {
        "backbone_residues": rf.num_residues,
        "rfdiffusion_ms": rf.elapsed_ms,
        "sequences_designed": mpnn.num_sequences,
        "sequences": mpnn.sequences,
        "duration_s": round(dur, 2),
        "success": rf.success and mpnn.success,
    }

    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps(out, indent=2, default=str))
        return 0 if out["success"] else 1

    print(f"  Backbone: {green(str(rf.num_residues))} residues  |  RFdiffusion: {rf.elapsed_ms:.0f}ms")
    if mpnn.success:
        print(f"  ProteinMPNN designed {green(str(mpnn.num_sequences))} sequences")
        for seq in mpnn.sequences[:3]:
            s = seq.get("sequence", "")[:60]
            sc = seq.get("score", 0)
            print(f"    score={sc:.4f}  {dim(s)}...")
    print(dim(f"  {dur:.1f}s elapsed\n"))
    return 0


def cmd_health(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Check which NIM endpoints are reachable."""
    _load_env()
    nim = _nim()
    if not nim.available:
        msg = "NVIDIA_API_KEY not set"
        if args.json:
            print(json.dumps({"error": msg, "endpoints": {}}))
        else:
            print(red(f"  {msg}. Set it in .env or environment.\n"))
        return 1

    print(bold(cyan("  NIM Endpoint Health Check\n")) if not args.json else "", end="")
    t0 = time.time()
    status = nim.health_check()
    dur = time.time() - t0

    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps({"endpoints": status, "duration_s": round(dur, 2)}))
        return 0

    rows = []
    for name, ok in status.items():
        sym = green("READY") if ok else red("DOWN ")
        rows.append([f" {name}", f" {sym}"])
    print(table([" Endpoint", " Status"], rows, [16, 10]))
    total = sum(status.values())
    print(dim(f"  {total}/{len(status)} endpoints ready  |  {dur:.1f}s\n"))
    return 0


def cmd_models(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """List available NIM biology models."""
    _load_env()
    from src.models.nim_client import ENDPOINTS
    has_key = bool(os.environ.get("NVIDIA_API_KEY"))

    models_info = [
        ("ESMFold",      "esmfold",      "Protein structure prediction"),
        ("Evo2-40b",     "evo2",         "Genomic sequence generation"),
        ("Boltz-2",      "boltz2",       "Protein-ligand binding prediction"),
        ("MolMIM",       "molmim",       "Novel molecule generation"),
        ("RFdiffusion",  "rfdiffusion",  "De novo protein backbone design"),
        ("ProteinMPNN",  "proteinmpnn",  "Sequence design for backbones"),
        ("DiffDock",     "diffdock",     "Molecular docking (3D poses)"),
    ]

    if args.json:
        out = [{"name": n, "key": k, "url": ENDPOINTS[k], "description": d,
                "api_key_set": has_key} for n, k, d in models_info]
        print(json.dumps(out, indent=2))
        return 0

    print(bold(cyan("\n  NVIDIA NIM Biology Models")) + f"  {badge('API Key', has_key)}\n")
    rows = [[f" {n}", f" {k}", f" {d}"] for n, k, d in models_info]
    print(table([" Model", " Key", " Description"], rows, [16, 14, 42]))
    print()
    return 0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Argument parser
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="resistai",
        description="ResistAI -- AMR drug discovery CLI powered by NVIDIA NIM",
    )
    p.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output as JSON")
    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest="command", help="Command to run")

    def _sub(name: str, **kw) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
        sp = sub.add_parser(name, **kw)
        sp.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", default=False, help="Output as JSON")
        return sp

    # run
    r = _sub("run", help="Run the full drug discovery pipeline")
    r.add_argument("--pathogen", default="acinetobacter_baumannii", help="Target pathogen")
    r.add_argument("--max-drugs", type=int, default=100, help="Max compounds to screen")
    r.add_argument("--novel", action="store_true", help="Enable novel molecule generation")

    # predict
    pr = _sub("predict", help="Predict protein-ligand binding (Boltz-2)")
    pr.add_argument("--sequence", required=True, help="Protein amino acid sequence")
    pr.add_argument("--smiles", required=True, help="Ligand SMILES string")

    # fold
    f = _sub("fold", help="Fold a protein sequence (ESMFold)")
    f.add_argument("--sequence", required=True, help="Amino acid sequence")
    f.add_argument("--output", default=None, help="Output PDB file path")

    # generate
    g = _sub("generate", help="Generate novel molecules (MolMIM)")
    g.add_argument("--seed", required=True, help="Seed SMILES string")
    g.add_argument("--n", type=int, default=5, help="Number of molecules")
    g.add_argument("--optimize", default="QED", help="Property to optimize")

    # dock
    d = _sub("dock", help="Dock molecule into protein (DiffDock)")
    d.add_argument("--sequence", required=True, help="Protein amino acid sequence")
    d.add_argument("--smiles", required=True, help="Ligand SMILES string")
    d.add_argument("--poses", type=int, default=5, help="Number of docking poses")

    # design-binder
    db = _sub("design-binder", help="Design protein binder (RFdiffusion + ProteinMPNN)")
    db.add_argument("--sequence", required=True, help="Target protein sequence")
    db.add_argument("--contigs", default="10-30/A1-60/10-30", help="Contig specification")

    # health / models
    _sub("health", help="Check NIM endpoint availability")
    _sub("models", help="List available NIM biology models")

    return p


_DISPATCH = {
    "run": cmd_run,
    "predict": cmd_predict,
    "fold": cmd_fold,
    "generate": cmd_generate,
    "dock": cmd_dock,
    "design-binder": cmd_design_binder,
    "health": cmd_health,
    "models": cmd_models,
}


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = build_parser()
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    # --json may appear before or after subcommand; ensure it's True if set anywhere
    raw = argv if argv is not None else sys.argv[1:]
    if "--json" in raw:
        args.json = True

    if not args.command:
        parser.print_help()
        return 1

    handler = _DISPATCH.get(args.command)
    if not handler:
        parser.print_help()
        return 1

    try:
        return handler(args)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print(yellow("\n  Interrupted."))
        return 130
    except Exception as exc:
        if getattr(args, "json", False):
            print(json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}))
        else:
            print(red(f"\n  Error: {exc}\n"))
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

--- END OF FILE: .\src\cli.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\comparison.py ---
"""Molecule Comparison Module.

Compare drug candidates across multiple dimensions with radar charts,
ranked tables, and summary statistics.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

try:
    import plotly.graph_objects as go
except ImportError:
    go = None  # type: ignore[assignment]

try:
    import pandas as pd
except ImportError:
    pd = None  # type: ignore[assignment]

# Spectrum palette matching the app dark theme
_COLORS = [
    "#00C4B4",  # teal (primary)
    "#7C8CF8",  # soft blue
    "#F43F5E",  # rose
    "#F59E0B",  # amber
    "#FB923C",  # orange
    "#94A3B8",  # slate
    "#E2E8F0",  # off-white
    "#1E2D4A",  # blue-grey
]

_BG = "#0A0F1E"

# Dimensions available for comparison and ranking
DIMENSIONS = ["Binding", "Safety", "Overall", "Druggability", "Selectivity"]


def _parse_pct(value: str | float) -> float:
    """Convert a percentage string like '87%' or float to 0-1 float."""
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return float(value) if value <= 1.0 else value / 100.0
    s = str(value).strip().rstrip("%")
    try:
        v = float(s)
        return v / 100.0 if v > 1.0 else v
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return 0.0


def _ensure_dimensions(candidate: dict) -> dict:
    """Ensure candidate has all dimensions, synthesising missing ones."""
    c = dict(candidate)
    binding = _parse_pct(c.get("binding", c.get("Binding", 0)))
    safety = _parse_pct(c.get("safety", c.get("Safety", 0)))
    overall = _parse_pct(c.get("overall", c.get("Overall", 0)))

    c.setdefault("Binding", binding)
    c.setdefault("Safety", safety)
    c.setdefault("Overall", overall)
    # Derive druggability and selectivity from available data
    c.setdefault("Druggability", round(0.6 * binding + 0.4 * safety, 3))
    c.setdefault("Selectivity", round(0.4 * binding + 0.6 * (1.0 - abs(binding - safety)), 3))
    return c


def compare_candidates(candidates: list[dict]) -> dict:
    """Compare multiple drug candidates on all dimensions.

    Each candidate dict has: name, smiles, binding, safety, overall, moa, phase.
    Returns comparison data with rankings per dimension.
    """
    enriched = [_ensure_dimensions(c) for c in candidates]
    rankings: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
        rankings[dim] = rank_by_dimension(enriched, dim)

    best_per_dim = {
        dim: ranked[0]["name"] if ranked else "N/A"
        for dim, ranked in rankings.items()
    }

    return {
        "candidates": enriched,
        "rankings": rankings,
        "best_per_dimension": best_per_dim,
        "dimensions": DIMENSIONS,
        "count": len(enriched),
    }


def generate_radar_chart(candidates: list[dict]) -> Any:
    """Create a Plotly radar chart comparing candidates.

    Axes: Binding, Safety, Overall, Druggability, Selectivity.
    One trace per candidate, overlaid.
    Dark theme matching the app (#0A0F1E bg, teal accent).
    """
    if go is None:
        return None

    enriched = [_ensure_dimensions(c) for c in candidates]
    axes = DIMENSIONS
    fig = go.Figure()

    for i, cand in enumerate(enriched):
        name = cand.get("name", cand.get("💊 Drug", f"Candidate {i + 1}"))
        values = [_parse_pct(cand.get(dim, 0)) for dim in axes]
        # Close the polygon
        values_closed = values + [values[0]]
        axes_closed = axes + [axes[0]]
        color = _COLORS[i % len(_COLORS)]

        fig.add_trace(go.Scatterpolar(
            r=values_closed,
            theta=axes_closed,
            fill="toself",
            fillcolor=color.replace(")", ",0.10)").replace("rgb", "rgba")
            if "rgb" in color else f"rgba({int(color[1:3], 16)},{int(color[3:5], 16)},{int(color[5:7], 16)},0.10)",
            line=dict(color=color, width=2),
            name=name,
        ))

    fig.update_layout(
        polar=dict(
            bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
            radialaxis=dict(
                visible=True, range=[0, 1], gridcolor="#1E2D4A",
                tickvals=[0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0],
                ticktext=["25%", "50%", "75%", "100%"],
            ),
            angularaxis=dict(gridcolor="#1E2D4A"),
        ),
        paper_bgcolor=_BG,
        plot_bgcolor=_BG,
        font=dict(color="#E2E8F0", size=12),
        legend=dict(
            font=dict(size=11, color="#E2E8F0"),
            bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0.3)",
            bordercolor="#1E2D4A",
            borderwidth=1,
        ),
        margin=dict(l=80, r=80, t=60, b=60),
        title=dict(
            text="Drug Candidate Comparison",
            font=dict(color="#00C4B4", size=16),
        ),
    )
    return fig


def generate_comparison_table(candidates: list[dict]) -> Any:
    """Build a comparison table as a list-of-dicts (for gr.Dataframe).

    Returns a pandas DataFrame if available, else a list of dicts.
    """
    enriched = [_ensure_dimensions(c) for c in candidates]
    rows = []
    for i, c in enumerate(enriched):
        name = c.get("name", c.get("💊 Drug", f"Candidate {i + 1}"))
        rows.append({
            "Rank": i + 1,
            "Drug": name,
            "Binding": f"{_parse_pct(c.get('Binding', 0)):.0%}",
            "Safety": f"{_parse_pct(c.get('Safety', 0)):.0%}",
            "Overall": f"{_parse_pct(c.get('Overall', 0)):.0%}",
            "Druggability": f"{_parse_pct(c.get('Druggability', 0)):.0%}",
            "Selectivity": f"{_parse_pct(c.get('Selectivity', 0)):.0%}",
            "MoA": c.get("moa", c.get("Why It Works", "")),
        })
    if pd is not None:
        return pd.DataFrame(rows)
    return rows


def rank_by_dimension(candidates: list[dict], dimension: str) -> list[dict]:
    """Re-rank candidates by a specific dimension.

    Args:
        candidates: List of candidate dicts (already enriched or raw).
        dimension: One of DIMENSIONS.

    Returns:
        Sorted list of dicts with name, score, and rank.
    """
    enriched = [_ensure_dimensions(c) for c in candidates]
    scored = []
    for c in enriched:
        name = c.get("name", c.get("💊 Drug", "Unknown"))
        score = _parse_pct(c.get(dimension, 0))
        scored.append({"name": name, "score": score, **c})

    scored.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"], reverse=True)
    for i, entry in enumerate(scored, 1):
        entry["rank"] = i
    return scored

--- END OF FILE: .\src\comparison.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\visualization.py ---
"""3D Molecular Visualization using 3Dmol.js via iframe srcdoc.

Gradio 6 strips <script> tags from gr.HTML. The only reliable way to run
JavaScript is via an iframe with srcdoc. PDB data is base64-encoded to
avoid escaping issues with large molecular data.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import html as html_mod
import uuid
from typing import Optional

_3DMOL_CDN = "https://3Dmol.org/build/3Dmol-min.js"
_BG = "#0A0F1E"


def _uid() -> str:
    return uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]


def _b64(text: str) -> str:
    return base64.b64encode(text.encode()).decode()


def _iframe(inner_body: str, inner_script: str, height: int = 450) -> str:
    """Build a self-contained HTML document and embed it in an iframe."""
    doc = (
        "<!DOCTYPE html>"
        "<html><head>"
        f'<script src="{_3DMOL_CDN}"></script>'
        f"<style>*{{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}}"
        f"body{{background:{_BG};overflow:hidden}}</style>"
        "</head><body>"
        f"{inner_body}"
        f"<script>{inner_script}</script>"
        "</body></html>"
    )
    escaped = html_mod.escape(doc, quote=True)
    return (
        f'<iframe srcdoc="{escaped}" '
        f'style="width:100%;height:{height}px;border:1px solid #1E2D4A;'
        f'border-radius:8px;background:{_BG}" '
        f'sandbox="allow-scripts" '
        f'loading="lazy"></iframe>'
    )


def render_protein_pdb(pdb_string: str, title: str = "") -> str:
    """Render a PDB structure in 3D with cartoon representation."""
    if not pdb_string or not pdb_string.strip():
        return "<p style='color:#ff6b6b'>No PDB data provided.</p>"

    pdb_b64 = _b64(pdb_string)
    title_html = (
        f"<h4 style='color:#E2E8F0;margin:4px 0'>{title}</h4>" if title else ""
    )
    body = '<div id="v" style="width:100%;height:100%;position:absolute;top:0;left:0"></div>'
    script = (
        f'var pdb=atob("{pdb_b64}");'
        f'var v=$3Dmol.createViewer("v",{{backgroundColor:"{_BG}"}});'
        'v.addModel(pdb,"pdb");'
        'v.setStyle({},{cartoon:{color:"spectrum"}});'
        'v.addSurface($3Dmol.SurfaceType.VDW,{opacity:0.06,color:"white"});'
        'v.zoomTo();v.render();v.zoom(1.1,800);'
    )
    return f"{title_html}\n{_iframe(body, script)}"


def render_docking_pose(
    protein_pdb: str, ligand_sdf: str, title: str = ""
) -> str:
    """Render protein with a docked ligand overlay."""
    if not protein_pdb or not protein_pdb.strip():
        return "<p style='color:#ff6b6b'>No protein PDB data provided.</p>"
    if not ligand_sdf or not ligand_sdf.strip():
        return render_protein_pdb(protein_pdb, title=title or "Protein (no ligand)")

    pdb_b64 = _b64(protein_pdb)
    sdf_b64 = _b64(ligand_sdf)
    title_html = (
        f"<h4 style='color:#E2E8F0;margin:4px 0'>{title}</h4>" if title else ""
    )
    body = '<div id="v" style="width:100%;height:100%;position:absolute;top:0;left:0"></div>'
    script = (
        f'var pdb=atob("{pdb_b64}");var sdf=atob("{sdf_b64}");'
        f'var v=$3Dmol.createViewer("v",{{backgroundColor:"{_BG}"}});'
        'v.addModel(pdb,"pdb");'
        'v.setStyle({model:0},{cartoon:{color:"spectrum",opacity:0.85}});'
        'v.addModel(sdf,"sdf");'
        'v.setStyle({model:1},{stick:{colorscheme:"greenCarbon",radius:0.2}});'
        'v.addSurface($3Dmol.SurfaceType.VDW,{opacity:0.05,color:"white"},{model:0});'
        'v.zoomTo({model:1});v.zoom(0.8,600);v.render();'
    )
    return f"{title_html}\n{_iframe(body, script)}"


def render_molecule_2d(smiles: str) -> Optional[str]:
    """Generate an SVG image of a molecule from its SMILES string."""
    if not smiles or not smiles.strip():
        return None
    try:
        from rdkit import Chem
        from rdkit.Chem import Draw

        mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)
        if mol is None:
            return None
        drawer = Draw.MolDraw2DSVG(400, 300)
        drawer.drawOptions().clearBackground = False
        drawer.DrawMolecule(mol)
        drawer.FinishDrawing()
        return drawer.GetDrawingText()
    except (ImportError, Exception):
        return None


def render_comparison(
    pdb1: str, pdb2: str, title1: str = "Structure A", title2: str = "Structure B"
) -> str:
    """Render two protein structures side by side for comparison."""
    parts: list[str] = ['<div style="display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap">']
    for pdb_data, label in [(pdb1, title1), (pdb2, title2)]:
        if not pdb_data or not pdb_data.strip():
            parts.append(
                f"<div style='flex:1;min-width:300px'>"
                f"<h4 style='color:#E2E8F0;text-align:center'>{label}</h4>"
                f"<p style='color:#ff6b6b;text-align:center'>No PDB data.</p></div>"
            )
            continue
        pdb_b64 = _b64(pdb_data)
        body = '<div id="v" style="width:100%;height:100%;position:absolute;top:0;left:0"></div>'
        script = (
            f'var pdb=atob("{pdb_b64}");'
            f'var v=$3Dmol.createViewer("v",{{backgroundColor:"{_BG}"}});'
            'v.addModel(pdb,"pdb");'
            'v.setStyle({},{cartoon:{color:"spectrum"}});'
            'v.zoomTo();v.render();'
        )
        parts.append(
            f'<div style="flex:1;min-width:300px">'
            f'<h4 style="color:#E2E8F0;text-align:center">{label}</h4>'
            f'{_iframe(body, script, height=380)}</div>'
        )
    parts.append("</div>")
    return "\n".join(parts)

"""AMR-AI: AI Drug Discovery Platform for Antimicrobial Resistance"""
__version__ = "0.1.0"

--- START OF FILE: .\src\api\server.py ---
"""ResistAI FastAPI server for AMR drug discovery pipeline.

Exposes NVIDIA NIM biology models and the full pipeline orchestrator
as a production-ready REST API with auth, CORS, timing, and health checks.

Run with: uvicorn src.api.server:app --port 8000
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
import time
from dataclasses import asdict
from typing import Any, Optional

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, Security
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from fastapi.security import APIKeyHeader
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Pydantic request/response models
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class PipelineRequest(BaseModel):
    pathogen: str = Field(..., description="Pathogen identifier, e.g. acinetobacter_baumannii")
    max_drugs: int = Field(100, ge=1, le=500, description="Max compounds to screen per target")
    generate_novel: bool = Field(True, description="Run MolMIM novel molecule generation")


class BindingRequest(BaseModel):
    protein_sequence: str = Field(..., min_length=10, description="Amino acid sequence")
    ligand_smiles: str = Field(..., min_length=1, description="Ligand SMILES string")


class StructureRequest(BaseModel):
    sequence: str = Field(..., min_length=10, description="Amino acid sequence")


class GenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    seed_smiles: str = Field(..., min_length=1, description="Seed molecule SMILES")
    num_molecules: int = Field(5, ge=1, le=50, description="Number of molecules to generate")
    optimize: str = Field("QED", description="Property to optimize (QED, logP, etc.)")


class DNAGenerateRequest(BaseModel):
    sequence: str = Field(..., min_length=5, description="Input DNA sequence (A/T/G/C)")
    num_tokens: int = Field(256, ge=1, le=2048, description="Nucleotide tokens to generate")


class DockRequest(BaseModel):
    protein_pdb: str = Field(..., min_length=10, description="Protein PDB structure string")
    ligand_smiles: str = Field(..., min_length=1, description="Ligand SMILES string")
    num_poses: int = Field(5, ge=1, le=20, description="Number of docking poses")


class BinderRequest(BaseModel):
    target_sequence: str = Field(..., min_length=10, description="Target protein sequence")
    contigs: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="RFdiffusion contig specification")


class APIResponse(BaseModel):
    success: bool
    data: Any = None
    error: Optional[str] = None
    elapsed_ms: float = 0.0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# App setup
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

app = FastAPI(
    title="ResistAI API",
    description="AMR drug discovery pipeline powered by NVIDIA NIM biology models",
    version="1.0.0",
    docs_url="/docs",
    redoc_url="/redoc",
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Auth
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_api_key_header = APIKeyHeader(name="X-API-Key", auto_error=False)
_REQUIRED_KEY = os.environ.get("RESISTAI_API_KEY", "")


async def verify_api_key(api_key: Optional[str] = Security(_api_key_header)) -> Optional[str]:
    """Validate X-API-Key header. Skip check if RESISTAI_API_KEY is not configured."""
    if not _REQUIRED_KEY:
        return api_key
    if api_key != _REQUIRED_KEY:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid or missing API key")
    return api_key


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Timing middleware
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@app.middleware("http")
async def timing_middleware(request: Request, call_next):
    t0 = time.time()
    response = await call_next(request)
    elapsed = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
    response.headers["X-Response-Time-Ms"] = f"{elapsed:.1f}"
    return response


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Lazy-loaded clients (avoid import errors at module level)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_nim_client = None
_orchestrator = None


def _get_nim():
    global _nim_client
    if _nim_client is None:
        from src.models.nim_client import NIMClient
        _nim_client = NIMClient()
    return _nim_client


def _get_orchestrator():
    global _orchestrator
    if _orchestrator is None:
        from src.pipeline.orchestrator import PipelineOrchestrator
        _orchestrator = PipelineOrchestrator()
    return _orchestrator


def _result_to_dict(result: Any) -> dict:
    """Convert a dataclass result to a plain dict."""
    if hasattr(result, "to_dict"):
        return result.to_dict()
    return asdict(result)


def _timed(func, *args, **kwargs) -> tuple[Any, float]:
    """Call func and return (result, elapsed_ms)."""
    t0 = time.time()
    result = func(*args, **kwargs)
    return result, (time.time() - t0) * 1000


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Startup
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@app.on_event("startup")
async def startup_check():
    nim = _get_nim()
    if nim.available:
        logger.info("NIM client available with API key")
    else:
        logger.warning("NIM client unavailable -- endpoints will use fallback/mock data")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Endpoints
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@app.post("/v1/pipeline/run", response_model=APIResponse)
async def run_pipeline(req: PipelineRequest, _key: str = Security(verify_api_key)):
    """Run the full AMR drug discovery pipeline for a pathogen."""
    try:
        orch = _get_orchestrator()
        result, elapsed = _timed(
            orch.run_full_pipeline,
            pathogen_name=req.pathogen,
            max_drugs=req.max_drugs,
            generate_novel=req.generate_novel,
        )
        return APIResponse(success=True, data=result.to_dict(), elapsed_ms=round(elapsed, 1))
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc))
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Pipeline failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc))


@app.post("/v1/predict/binding", response_model=APIResponse)
async def predict_binding(req: BindingRequest, _key: str = Security(verify_api_key)):
    """Predict protein-ligand binding affinity using Boltz-2."""
    nim = _get_nim()
    if not nim.available:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="NIM client unavailable (no API key)")
    try:
        result, elapsed = _timed(nim.predict_binding, req.protein_sequence, req.ligand_smiles)
        return APIResponse(success=result.success, data=_result_to_dict(result), elapsed_ms=round(elapsed, 1))
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Binding prediction failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc))


@app.post("/v1/predict/structure", response_model=APIResponse)
async def predict_structure(req: StructureRequest, _key: str = Security(verify_api_key)):
    """Predict protein 3D structure using ESMFold."""
    nim = _get_nim()
    if not nim.available:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="NIM client unavailable (no API key)")
    try:
        result, elapsed = _timed(nim.fold_protein, req.sequence)
        return APIResponse(success=result.success, data=_result_to_dict(result), elapsed_ms=round(elapsed, 1))
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Structure prediction failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc))


@app.post("/v1/generate/molecules", response_model=APIResponse)
async def generate_molecules(req: GenerateRequest, _key: str = Security(verify_api_key)):
    """Generate novel optimized molecules using MolMIM."""
    nim = _get_nim()
    if not nim.available:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="NIM client unavailable (no API key)")
    try:
        result, elapsed = _timed(
            nim.generate_molecules,
            seed_smiles=req.seed_smiles,
            num_molecules=req.num_molecules,
            property_name=req.optimize,
        )
        return APIResponse(success=result.success, data=_result_to_dict(result), elapsed_ms=round(elapsed, 1))
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Molecule generation failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc))


@app.post("/v1/generate/dna", response_model=APIResponse)
async def generate_dna(req: DNAGenerateRequest, _key: str = Security(verify_api_key)):
    """Generate DNA sequences using Evo2-40b."""
    nim = _get_nim()
    if not nim.available:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="NIM client unavailable (no API key)")
    try:
        result, elapsed = _timed(nim.generate_dna, req.sequence, req.num_tokens)
        return APIResponse(success=result.success, data=_result_to_dict(result), elapsed_ms=round(elapsed, 1))
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("DNA generation failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc))


@app.post("/v1/dock", response_model=APIResponse)
async def dock_molecule(req: DockRequest, _key: str = Security(verify_api_key)):
    """Predict 3D binding pose using DiffDock."""
    nim = _get_nim()
    if not nim.available:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="NIM client unavailable (no API key)")
    try:
        result, elapsed = _timed(
            nim.dock_molecule,
            protein_pdb=req.protein_pdb,
            ligand_smiles=req.ligand_smiles,
            num_poses=req.num_poses,
        )
        return APIResponse(success=result.success, data=_result_to_dict(result), elapsed_ms=round(elapsed, 1))
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Docking failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc))


@app.post("/v1/design/binder", response_model=APIResponse)
async def design_binder(req: BinderRequest, _key: str = Security(verify_api_key)):
    """Design a protein binder using RFdiffusion + ProteinMPNN.

    First folds the target sequence with ESMFold, then designs a binder
    backbone with RFdiffusion, and finally designs sequences with ProteinMPNN.
    """
    nim = _get_nim()
    if not nim.available:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="NIM client unavailable (no API key)")
    try:
        t0 = time.time()

        # Step 1: Fold target
        fold = nim.fold_protein(req.target_sequence)
        if not fold.success:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="ESMFold failed to fold target sequence")

        # Step 2: Design binder backbone
        n_res = min(len(req.target_sequence), 60)
        contigs = req.contigs or f"10-30/A1-{n_res}/10-30"
        backbone = nim.design_binder(fold.pdb_string, contigs=contigs)
        if not backbone.success:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="RFdiffusion binder design failed")

        # Step 3: Design sequences for backbone
        sequences = nim.design_sequence(backbone.pdb_string)

        elapsed = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        data = {
            "backbone_residues": backbone.num_residues,
            "backbone_elapsed_ms": backbone.elapsed_ms,
            "sequences": _result_to_dict(sequences) if sequences.success else None,
            "contigs_used": contigs,
        }
        return APIResponse(success=sequences.success, data=data, elapsed_ms=round(elapsed, 1))
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Binder design failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc))


@app.get("/v1/health", response_model=APIResponse)
async def health_check():
    """Check availability of all NIM endpoints."""
    nim = _get_nim()
    if not nim.available:
        return APIResponse(
            success=False,
            data={"nim_available": False, "endpoints": {}},
            error="NIM client unavailable (no API key)",
        )
    try:
        result, elapsed = _timed(nim.health_check)
        all_ok = all(result.values())
        return APIResponse(
            success=all_ok,
            data={"nim_available": True, "endpoints": result},
            elapsed_ms=round(elapsed, 1),
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Health check failed")
        return APIResponse(success=False, error=str(exc))


@app.get("/v1/models", response_model=APIResponse)
async def list_models():
    """List available NIM models and their status."""
    from src.models.nim_client import ENDPOINTS

    nim = _get_nim()
    models = []
    for name, url in ENDPOINTS.items():
        models.append({
            "name": name,
            "url": url,
            "available": nim.available,
        })
    return APIResponse(
        success=True,
        data={
            "nim_configured": nim.available,
            "model_count": len(models),
            "models": models,
        },
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global exception handler
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@app.exception_handler(Exception)
async def global_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: Exception):
    logger.exception("Unhandled error on %s %s", request.method, request.url.path)
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=500,
        content={"success": False, "error": str(exc), "data": None, "elapsed_ms": 0.0},
    )

"""Drug library manager with ChEMBL and Broad Repurposing Hub integration.

import io
import logging
from typing import Optional

import httpx

class DrugLibrary:
    """Manages retrieval and caching of approved drug compound data."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._settings = get_settings()
        self._approved_cache: Optional[list[dict[str, str]]] = None
        self._repurposing_cache: Optional[list[dict[str, str]]] = None
        self._smiles_index: dict[str, str] = {}

    # -- public API -----------------------------------------------------------

    async def fetch_approved_drugs(self, limit: int = 500) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        """Fetch phase-4 approved drugs from ChEMBL.

        Falls back to hardcoded antibiotics on network failure.
        """
        if self._approved_cache is not None:
            return self._approved_cache

        url = (
            f"{self._settings.chembl_base}/molecule.json"
            f"?max_phase=4"
            f"&molecule_structures__canonical_smiles__isnull=false"
            f"&limit={limit}&format=json"
        )
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0) as client:
                resp = await client.get(url)
                resp.raise_for_status()
            data = resp.json()
            molecules = data.get("molecules", [])
            results: list[dict[str, str]] = []
            for mol in molecules:
                structs = mol.get("molecule_structures") or {}
                smiles = structs.get("canonical_smiles", "")
                if smiles:
                    entry = {
                        "name": mol.get("pref_name") or mol.get("molecule_chembl_id", ""),
                        "smiles": smiles,
                        "chembl_id": mol.get("molecule_chembl_id", ""),
                        "phase": str(mol.get("max_phase", "")),
                    }
                    results.append(entry)
            self._approved_cache = results if results else _FALLBACK_ANTIBIOTICS
        except Exception:
            logger.warning("ChEMBL fetch failed; using fallback antibiotic set")
            self._approved_cache = _FALLBACK_ANTIBIOTICS

        self._index_smiles(self._approved_cache)
        return self._approved_cache

    async def fetch_drug_repurposing_hub(self) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        """Download and parse the Broad Drug Repurposing Hub TSV."""
        if self._repurposing_cache is not None:
            return self._repurposing_cache

        url = self._settings.drug_repurposing_hub_url
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=60.0) as client:
                resp = await client.get(url)
                resp.raise_for_status()
            text = resp.text
            # Skip comment lines starting with '!'
            lines = [ln for ln in text.splitlines() if not ln.startswith("!")]
            reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO("\n".join(lines)), delimiter="\t")
            results: list[dict[str, str]] = []
            for row in reader:
                smiles = row.get("smiles", "").strip()
                if smiles:
                    results.append({
                        "name": row.get("pert_iname", row.get("name", "")),
                        "smiles": smiles,
                        "clinical_phase": row.get("clinical_phase", ""),
                        "moa": row.get("moa", ""),
                        "disease_area": row.get("disease_area", ""),
                    })
            self._repurposing_cache = results
        except Exception:
            logger.warning("Repurposing Hub fetch failed; returning empty list")
            self._repurposing_cache = []

        self._index_smiles(self._repurposing_cache)
        return self._repurposing_cache

    def get_drug_smiles(self, drug_name: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Look up SMILES by drug name (case-insensitive).

        Searches cached approved drugs and repurposing hub data.
        Falls back to the hardcoded antibiotic set.
        """
        key = drug_name.strip().lower()
        if key in self._smiles_index:
            return self._smiles_index[key]
        # Fallback scan of hardcoded list
        for entry in _FALLBACK_ANTIBIOTICS:
            if entry["name"].lower() == key:
                return entry["smiles"]
        return None

    # -- internals ------------------------------------------------------------

    def _index_smiles(self, drugs: list[dict[str, str]]) -> None:
        for entry in drugs:
            name = entry.get("name", "").strip().lower()
            smiles = entry.get("smiles", "")
            if name and smiles:
                self._smiles_index[name] = smiles

--- START OF FILE: .\src\data\pathogens.py ---
"""Pathogen data manager for ESKAPE organisms.

Loads pathogen metadata from the central settings.yaml and exposes
convenient lookup methods for targets, PDB codes, UniProt IDs, and
WHO priority levels.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from src.utils.config import get_settings


class PathogenManager:
    """Provides access to ESKAPE pathogen data loaded from configuration."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._pathogens: dict[str, Any] = get_settings().eskape_pathogens

    # -- public API -----------------------------------------------------------

    def list_pathogens(self) -> list[str]:
        """Return a sorted list of available pathogen identifiers."""
        return sorted(self._pathogens.keys())

    def get_pathogen(self, name: str) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Return full pathogen info by identifier.

        Args:
            name: Pathogen key, e.g. ``"acinetobacter_baumannii"``.

        Returns:
            Dict with keys: name, who_priority, resistance, proteome_id,
            key_targets.  ``None`` if the pathogen is not found.
        """
        return self._pathogens.get(name)

    def get_target(
        self, pathogen: str, target_name: str
    ) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Return a specific drug-target entry for a pathogen.

        Args:
            pathogen: Pathogen key.
            target_name: Target name, e.g. ``"NDM-1"``.

        Returns:
            Dict with keys like pdb, uniprot, function.
            ``None`` if not found.
        """
        pathogen_data = self._pathogens.get(pathogen)
        if pathogen_data is None:
            return None
        for target in pathogen_data.get("key_targets", []):
            if target["name"] == target_name:
                return target
        return None

    def get_pdb_codes(self, pathogen: str) -> list[str]:
        """Return all PDB codes for a given pathogen's targets."""
        pathogen_data = self._pathogens.get(pathogen)
        if pathogen_data is None:
            return []
        return [
            t["pdb"]
            for t in pathogen_data.get("key_targets", [])
            if "pdb" in t
        ]

    def get_uniprot_ids(self, pathogen: str) -> list[str]:
        """Return all UniProt IDs for a given pathogen's targets."""
        pathogen_data = self._pathogens.get(pathogen)
        if pathogen_data is None:
            return []
        return [
            t["uniprot"]
            for t in pathogen_data.get("key_targets", [])
            if "uniprot" in t
        ]

    def get_who_priority(self, pathogen: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Return the WHO priority level for a pathogen."""
        pathogen_data = self._pathogens.get(pathogen)
        if pathogen_data is None:
            return None
        return pathogen_data.get("who_priority")

    def get_critical_pathogens(self) -> list[str]:
        """Return pathogen keys whose WHO priority is 'critical'."""
        return [
            key
            for key, data in self._pathogens.items()
            if data.get("who_priority") == "critical"
        ]

--- START OF FILE: .\src\data\pdb_fetcher.py ---
"""PDB structure and FASTA sequence fetcher.

Downloads macromolecular structures from RCSB PDB and protein sequences
from the UniProt REST API, caching files locally under data/structures/.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

import httpx

from src.utils.config import get_settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class PDBFetcher:
    """Fetches and caches PDB structures and FASTA sequences."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._settings = get_settings()
        self._structures_dir: Path = self._settings.structures_dir

    # -- public API -----------------------------------------------------------

    async def fetch_pdb(self, pdb_id: str) -> Optional[Path]:
        """Download a PDB file from RCSB and save locally.

        Args:
            pdb_id: Four-character PDB identifier (e.g. ``"3SPU"``).

        Returns:
            Path to the downloaded file, or ``None`` on failure.
        """
        pdb_id = pdb_id.strip().upper()
        dest = self._structures_dir / f"{pdb_id}.pdb"
        if dest.exists():
            logger.debug("PDB %s already cached at %s", pdb_id, dest)
            return dest

        url = f"{self._settings.pdb_base}/{pdb_id}.pdb"
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0) as client:
                resp = await client.get(url)
                resp.raise_for_status()
            dest.write_text(resp.text)
            logger.info("Downloaded PDB %s -> %s", pdb_id, dest)
            return dest
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Failed to fetch PDB %s: %s", pdb_id, exc)
            return None

    async def fetch_fasta(self, uniprot_id: str) -> Optional[Path]:
        """Download a FASTA file from UniProt REST API.

        Args:
            uniprot_id: UniProt accession (e.g. ``"C7C422"``).

        Returns:
            Path to the downloaded FASTA file, or ``None`` on failure.
        """
        uniprot_id = uniprot_id.strip()
        dest = self._structures_dir / f"{uniprot_id}.fasta"
        if dest.exists():
            logger.debug("FASTA %s already cached at %s", uniprot_id, dest)
            return dest

        url = f"{self._settings.uniprot_base}/uniprotkb/{uniprot_id}.fasta"
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0) as client:
                resp = await client.get(url)
                resp.raise_for_status()
            dest.write_text(resp.text)
            logger.info("Downloaded FASTA %s -> %s", uniprot_id, dest)
            return dest
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Failed to fetch FASTA %s: %s", uniprot_id, exc)
            return None

    async def get_structure_path(self, pdb_id: str) -> Optional[Path]:
        """Return local path to a PDB file, fetching if not cached.

        Args:
            pdb_id: Four-character PDB identifier.

        Returns:
            Path to the PDB file, or ``None`` if download fails.
        """
        pdb_id = pdb_id.strip().upper()
        dest = self._structures_dir / f"{pdb_id}.pdb"
        if dest.exists():
            return dest
        return await self.fetch_pdb(pdb_id)

    def list_cached(self) -> list[str]:
        """Return PDB IDs of all locally cached structure files."""
        return [
            p.stem
            for p in self._structures_dir.glob("*.pdb")
        ]

--- END OF FILE: .\src\data\pdb_fetcher.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\models\llm_client.py ---
"""Nebius AI Studio LLM client for AMR-AI reasoning tasks.

Uses the available LLMs (DeepSeek-R1, Qwen3, etc.) for:
- Target analysis and interpretation
- Drug mechanism explanation
- Safety assessment reasoning
- Report generation
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import json, logging, os
from typing import Any

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Best models for reasoning tasks on Nebius
_REASONING_MODEL = "deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1-0528"
_FAST_MODEL = "Qwen/Qwen3-235B-A22B-Instruct-2507"
_CODER_MODEL = "Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-fast"


class NebisLLM:
    """Client for Nebius AI Studio LLM inference."""

    def __init__(self, model: str = _FAST_MODEL) -> None:
        self._model = model
        self._api_key = os.environ.get("NEBIUS_API_KEY", "")
        if not self._api_key:
            try:
                env_path = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), ".env")
                if os.path.exists(env_path):
                    for line in open(env_path):
                        if line.startswith("NEBIUS_API_KEY="):
                            self._api_key = line.split("=", 1)[1].strip()
            except Exception:
                pass
        self._client = None
        self._available = False
        self._init()

    def _init(self) -> None:
        if not self._api_key:
            logger.warning("No NEBIUS_API_KEY; LLM features disabled")
            return
        try:
            from openai import OpenAI
            self._client = OpenAI(
                base_url="https://api.studio.nebius.com/v1/",
                api_key=self._api_key,
            )
            self._available = True
            logger.info("Nebius LLM client ready (model=%s)", self._model)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Failed to init LLM client: %s", e)

    @property
    def available(self) -> bool:
        return self._available

    def complete(self, prompt: str, system: str = "", max_tokens: int = 2048,
                 temperature: float = 0.3, json_mode: bool = False) -> str:
        if not self._available:
            return ""
        messages = []
        if system:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system})
        messages.append({"role": "user", "content": prompt})
        try:
            kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
                "model": self._model,
                "messages": messages,
                "max_tokens": max_tokens,
                "temperature": temperature,
            }
            if json_mode:
                kwargs["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}
            resp = self._client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
            return resp.choices[0].message.content or ""
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("LLM call failed: %s", e)
            return ""

    def complete_json(self, prompt: str, system: str = "",
                      max_tokens: int = 2048) -> dict | list | None:
        raw = self.complete(prompt, system, max_tokens, json_mode=True)
        if not raw:
            return None
        try:
            return json.loads(raw)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            start = raw.find("{")
            end = raw.rfind("}") + 1
            if start >= 0 and end > start:
                try:
                    return json.loads(raw[start:end])
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    pass
            return None

    def analyze_target(self, target_name: str, pathogen: str,
                       pdb_id: str = "", function: str = "") -> dict:
        prompt = f"""Analyze this antimicrobial resistance target for drug discovery:

Target: {target_name}
Pathogen: {pathogen}
PDB ID: {pdb_id}
Known function: {function}

Provide a JSON response with:
{{
  "druggability_assessment": "high/medium/low with reasoning",
  "binding_site_description": "description of the active site",
  "known_inhibitors": ["list of known inhibitor classes"],
  "resistance_mechanism": "how this target contributes to resistance",
  "therapeutic_strategy": "recommended approach to target this protein",
  "clinical_relevance": "why this target matters clinically",
  "confidence": 0.0-1.0
}}"""
        system = ("You are an expert computational biologist specializing in "
                  "antimicrobial resistance and drug discovery. Respond with valid JSON only.")
        result = self.complete_json(prompt, system)
        return result or {"error": "LLM analysis unavailable", "target": target_name}

    def analyze_drug_candidate(self, drug_name: str, smiles: str,
                                target_name: str, binding_score: float) -> dict:
        prompt = f"""Analyze this drug repurposing candidate for antimicrobial activity:

Drug: {drug_name}
SMILES: {smiles}
Target: {target_name}
Predicted binding affinity: {binding_score:.3f}

Provide a JSON response with:
{{
  "mechanism_of_action": "how this drug works against the target",
  "repurposing_rationale": "why this existing drug could work for AMR",
  "strengths": ["list of advantages"],
  "risks": ["list of potential issues"],
  "clinical_precedent": "any existing clinical evidence",
  "development_recommendation": "next steps for development",
  "regulatory_pathway": "505(b)(2) or other applicable pathway",
  "estimated_timeline": "years to potential approval"
}}"""
        system = ("You are a pharmaceutical scientist specializing in drug repurposing "
                  "for antimicrobial resistance. Respond with valid JSON only.")
        result = self.complete_json(prompt, system)
        return result or {"error": "LLM analysis unavailable", "drug": drug_name}

    def generate_safety_interpretation(self, drug_name: str, smiles: str,
                                        admet_data: dict) -> str:
        prompt = f"""Interpret this ADMET safety profile for a clinician audience:

Drug: {drug_name}
SMILES: {smiles}
ADMET Data: {json.dumps(admet_data, indent=2)}

Write a 3-4 sentence plain-English safety summary that a non-scientist can understand.
Include specific risks to watch for and an overall safety verdict."""
        system = "You are a clinical pharmacologist explaining drug safety to a medical team."
        return self.complete(prompt, system, max_tokens=500)

    def generate_executive_report(self, pathogen: str, targets: list,
                                   candidates: list, num_screened: int) -> str:
        prompt = f"""Write a concise executive summary for an AI drug discovery pipeline run:

Pathogen: {pathogen}
Targets identified: {len(targets)} ({', '.join(t.get('name', t) if isinstance(t, dict) else str(t) for t in targets[:5])})
Compounds screened: {num_screened}
Top candidates: {json.dumps(candidates[:5], indent=2, default=str)}

Write a 2-paragraph executive summary suitable for hackathon judges.
Focus on: clinical impact, speed vs traditional methods, regulatory pathway, next steps.
Be specific about the pathogen threat and why these candidates matter."""
        system = ("You are a biotech venture analyst writing for hackathon judges "
                  "who include both scientists and business strategists.")
        return self.complete(prompt, system, max_tokens=800)

--- START OF FILE: .\src\models\schemas.py ---
"""Shared data schemas for the AMR-AI pipeline.

Defines all dataclasses used across target discovery, drug screening,
safety prediction, and pipeline orchestration modules.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Optional


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Target Discovery
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class TargetResult:
    """A druggable protein target identified during target discovery."""

    name: str
    pdb_code: str
    uniprot_id: str = ""
    function: str = ""
    druggability_score: float = 0.0
    pathogen: str = ""
    resistance_mechanism: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> TargetResult:
        return cls(**{k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in cls.__dataclass_fields__})

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"Target({self.name}, PDB={self.pdb_code}, "
            f"druggability={self.druggability_score:.2f})"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Drug Screening
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class BindingResult:
    """Result of a single docking/binding affinity prediction."""

    drug_name: str
    smiles: str
    target_name: str
    binding_affinity: float = 0.0
    affinity_probability: float = 0.0
    docking_score: float = 0.0
    confidence: float = 0.0

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> BindingResult:
        return cls(**{k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in cls.__dataclass_fields__})

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"Binding({self.drug_name} -> {self.target_name}, "
            f"affinity={self.binding_affinity:.3f})"
        )


@dataclass
class ScreeningHit:
    """A drug that passed binding threshold during virtual screening."""

    rank: int
    drug_name: str
    smiles: str
    target_name: str
    binding_affinity: float = 0.0
    confidence: float = 0.0
    mechanism_of_action: str = ""
    clinical_phase: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> ScreeningHit:
        return cls(**{k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in cls.__dataclass_fields__})

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"Hit #{self.rank}: {self.drug_name} "
            f"(affinity={self.binding_affinity:.3f}, conf={self.confidence:.2f})"
        )


@dataclass
class ScreeningResults:
    """Aggregated results from a virtual drug screening run."""

    target_name: str
    compounds_screened: int = 0
    hits: list[ScreeningHit] = field(default_factory=list)
    threshold_used: float = 0.5
    model_used: str = "demo"

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        d = asdict(self)
        d["hits"] = [h.to_dict() if isinstance(h, ScreeningHit) else h for h in self.hits]
        return d

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> ScreeningResults:
        hits_raw = data.pop("hits", [])
        hits = [ScreeningHit.from_dict(h) if isinstance(h, dict) else h for h in hits_raw]
        filtered = {k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in cls.__dataclass_fields__}
        return cls(**filtered, hits=hits)

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"Screening({self.target_name}: {len(self.hits)} hits "
            f"from {self.compounds_screened} compounds)"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Safety Prediction
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class ADMETResult:
    """ADMET (Absorption, Distribution, Metabolism, Excretion, Toxicity) profile."""

    drug_name: str
    absorption: float = 0.0
    distribution: float = 0.0
    metabolism: float = 0.0
    excretion: float = 0.0
    toxicity_score: float = 0.0
    oral_bioavailability: bool = True
    bbb_penetration: bool = False
    herg_liability: bool = False

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> ADMETResult:
        return cls(**{k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in cls.__dataclass_fields__})

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return f"ADMET({self.drug_name}, tox={self.toxicity_score:.2f})"


@dataclass
class OffTarget:
    """A single predicted off-target interaction."""

    protein_name: str
    similarity: float = 0.0
    risk_level: str = "low"
    tissue: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> OffTarget:
        return cls(**{k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in cls.__dataclass_fields__})

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return f"OffTarget({self.protein_name}, risk={self.risk_level})"


@dataclass
class OffTargetReport:
    """Aggregated off-target interaction report for a compound."""

    drug_name: str
    off_targets: list[OffTarget] = field(default_factory=list)
    overall_risk: str = "low"

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        d = asdict(self)
        d["off_targets"] = [
            ot.to_dict() if isinstance(ot, OffTarget) else ot for ot in self.off_targets
        ]
        return d

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> OffTargetReport:
        ots_raw = data.pop("off_targets", [])
        ots = [OffTarget.from_dict(o) if isinstance(o, dict) else o for o in ots_raw]
        filtered = {k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in cls.__dataclass_fields__}
        return cls(**filtered, off_targets=ots)

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"OffTargetReport({self.drug_name}, "
            f"{len(self.off_targets)} off-targets, risk={self.overall_risk})"
        )


@dataclass
class SafetyReport:
    """Combined safety assessment for a drug candidate."""

    drug_name: str
    smiles: str = ""
    safety_score: float = 0.0
    admet: Optional[ADMETResult] = None
    off_target_report: Optional[OffTargetReport] = None
    flags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    recommendation: str = "proceed"

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        d = asdict(self)
        if self.admet:
            d["admet"] = self.admet.to_dict()
        if self.off_target_report:
            d["off_target_report"] = self.off_target_report.to_dict()
        return d

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> SafetyReport:
        admet_raw = data.pop("admet", None)
        ot_raw = data.pop("off_target_report", None)
        filtered = {k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in cls.__dataclass_fields__}
        obj = cls(**filtered)
        if isinstance(admet_raw, dict):
            obj.admet = ADMETResult.from_dict(admet_raw)
        if isinstance(ot_raw, dict):
            obj.off_target_report = OffTargetReport.from_dict(ot_raw)
        return obj

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"SafetyReport({self.drug_name}, score={self.safety_score:.2f}, "
            f"rec={self.recommendation})"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Pipeline Output
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class DrugCandidate:
    """A ranked drug candidate from the full pipeline."""

    rank: int
    drug_name: str
    smiles: str
    target_name: str
    binding_affinity: float = 0.0
    safety_score: float = 0.0
    overall_score: float = 0.0
    regulatory_pathway: str = "505(b)(2)"
    confidence: float = 0.0

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> DrugCandidate:
        return cls(**{k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in cls.__dataclass_fields__})

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"#{self.rank} {self.drug_name} "
            f"(score={self.overall_score:.3f}, safety={self.safety_score:.2f})"
        )


@dataclass
class PipelineResults:
    """Complete results from a full AMR-AI pipeline run."""

    pathogen: str
    targets: list[TargetResult] = field(default_factory=list)
    screening_results: list[ScreeningResults] = field(default_factory=list)
    safety_reports: list[SafetyReport] = field(default_factory=list)
    top_candidates: list[DrugCandidate] = field(default_factory=list)
    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "pathogen": self.pathogen,
            "targets": [t.to_dict() for t in self.targets],
            "screening_results": [s.to_dict() for s in self.screening_results],
            "safety_reports": [r.to_dict() for r in self.safety_reports],
            "top_candidates": [c.to_dict() for c in self.top_candidates],
            "metadata": self.metadata,
        }

    def to_json(self, indent: int = 2) -> str:
        return json.dumps(self.to_dict(), indent=indent)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> PipelineResults:
        return cls(
            pathogen=data["pathogen"],
            targets=[TargetResult.from_dict(t) for t in data.get("targets", [])],
            screening_results=[
                ScreeningResults.from_dict(s) for s in data.get("screening_results", [])
            ],
            safety_reports=[
                SafetyReport.from_dict(r) for r in data.get("safety_reports", [])
            ],
            top_candidates=[
                DrugCandidate.from_dict(c) for c in data.get("top_candidates", [])
            ],
            metadata=data.get("metadata", {}),
        )

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"PipelineResults({self.pathogen}: "
            f"{len(self.targets)} targets, "
            f"{len(self.top_candidates)} candidates)"
        )
