--- START OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\drug_screening\boltz2_client.py ---
# DEPRECATED: Use src.models.nim_client.NIMClient instead
"""Boltz-2 binding prediction client for AMR-AI drug screening.

Generates YAML inputs for Boltz-2 and calls the Nebius NIM API for
binding affinity predictions.  Falls back to a heuristic scoring
function when the API is unavailable.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import logging
import os
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional

import yaml

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

try:
    from openai import OpenAI

    OPENAI_SDK_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    OPENAI_SDK_AVAILABLE = False

from src.utils.chemistry import compute_descriptors, RDKIT_AVAILABLE

if RDKIT_AVAILABLE:
    from rdkit import Chem


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Data classes
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class BindingResult:
    """Result of a single binding prediction."""

    affinity_probability: float  # 0-1 binder score
    affinity_value: float  # log10 IC50 in uM
    confidence_score: float  # model confidence 0-1
    structure_path: Optional[str] = None  # path to predicted structure


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Known antibiotic scaffolds (SMARTS) for heuristic scoring
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_ANTIBIOTIC_SCAFFOLDS: list[str] = [
    "[#7]1~[#6]~[#6]~[#7]~[#6]~[#6]~1",  # pyrimidine
    "C1=CC=C2C(=C1)C(=O)C3=CC=CC=C3C2=O",  # anthraquinone
    "O=C1CC(=O)N([*])C1",  # beta-lactam core
    "[#6]1(=O)[#7][#6][#6][#16]1",  # thiazolidine (penam core)
    "c1cc2c(cc1)oc(=O)c(c2O)C(=O)",  # coumarin / fluoroquinolone-like
    "O=C(O)c1cn(*)c2cc(F)c(*)cc12",  # fluoroquinolone
]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Client
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class Boltz2Client:
    """Client for Boltz-2 binding affinity prediction via Nebius NIM."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: Optional[str] = None,
        base_url: str = "https://api.tokenfactory.nebius.com/v1",
        model: str = "boltz-2",
    ) -> None:
        self._api_key = api_key or os.getenv("NEBIUS_API_KEY", "")
        self._base_url = base_url
        self._model = model

    # -- public API ---------------------------------------------------------

    def predict_binding(
        self,
        protein_sequence: str,
        ligand_smiles: str,
        protein_id: str = "A",
        ligand_id: str = "B",
    ) -> BindingResult:
        """Predict binding affinity between a protein and a ligand.

        Tries the Nebius NIM Boltz-2 endpoint first; on any failure falls
        back to a heuristic scoring function.
        """
        yaml_input = self._build_yaml(
            protein_sequence, ligand_smiles, protein_id, ligand_id,
        )

        result = self._call_api(yaml_input, ligand_smiles)
        if result is not None:
            return result

        logger.info("Boltz-2 API unavailable; using heuristic fallback")
        return self._heuristic_score(ligand_smiles)

    def batch_predict(
        self,
        protein_sequence: str,
        ligand_smiles_list: list[str],
        protein_id: str = "A",
        ligand_id: str = "B",
    ) -> list[BindingResult]:
        """Run binding predictions for a list of ligands against one protein."""
        return [
            self.predict_binding(protein_sequence, smi, protein_id, ligand_id)
            for smi in ligand_smiles_list
        ]

    # -- YAML generation ----------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def _build_yaml(
        protein_sequence: str,
        ligand_smiles: str,
        protein_id: str,
        ligand_id: str,
    ) -> str:
        """Build the Boltz-2 input YAML document."""
        doc: dict = {
            "sequences": [
                {"protein": {"id": protein_id, "sequence": protein_sequence}},
                {"ligand": {"id": ligand_id, "smiles": ligand_smiles}},
            ],
            "properties": [
                {"affinity": {"chain_id": ligand_id}},
            ],
        }
        return yaml.dump(doc, default_flow_style=False, sort_keys=False)

    # -- API call -----------------------------------------------------------

    def _call_api(
        self, yaml_input: str, ligand_smiles: str,
    ) -> Optional[BindingResult]:
        """Attempt to call the Nebius NIM Boltz-2 endpoint."""
        if not OPENAI_SDK_AVAILABLE or not self._api_key:
            return None

        try:
            client = OpenAI(api_key=self._api_key, base_url=self._base_url)

            # Write YAML to a temp file for the files-based API
            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
                mode="w", suffix=".yaml", delete=False,
            ) as fh:
                fh.write(yaml_input)
                yaml_path = fh.name

            response = client.chat.completions.create(
                model=self._model,
                messages=[
                    {
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": yaml_input,
                    },
                ],
                temperature=0.0,
            )

            # Parse response — Boltz-2 returns affinity fields
            content = response.choices[0].message.content or ""
            return self._parse_api_response(content)

        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            logger.warning("Boltz-2 API call failed: %s", exc)
            return None

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_api_response(content: str) -> Optional[BindingResult]:
        """Parse a Boltz-2 API text response into a BindingResult."""
        try:
            data = yaml.safe_load(content)
            if isinstance(data, dict):
                return BindingResult(
                    affinity_probability=float(
                        data.get("affinity_probability_binary", 0.0),
                    ),
                    affinity_value=float(
                        data.get("affinity_value_log10_ic50", 0.0),
                    ),
                    confidence_score=float(
                        data.get("confidence", 0.0),
                    ),
                    structure_path=data.get("structure_path"),
                )
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            pass
        return None

    # -- Heuristic fallback -------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def _heuristic_score(ligand_smiles: str) -> BindingResult:
        """Generate a plausible mock score using molecular properties.

        Uses molecular weight, LogP, and antibiotic scaffold matching
        to produce a deterministic but reasonable score.
        """
        # Deterministic seed from SMILES
        digest = int(hashlib.sha256(ligand_smiles.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)
        base_noise = (digest % 1000) / 10000.0  # 0.0 - 0.1

        mw: float = 350.0
        log_p: float = 2.0
        scaffold_bonus: float = 0.0

        desc = compute_descriptors(ligand_smiles)
        if desc is not None:
            mw = desc["MW"]
            log_p = desc["LogP"]

        if RDKIT_AVAILABLE:
            mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(ligand_smiles)
            if mol is not None:
                for smarts in _ANTIBIOTIC_SCAFFOLDS:
                    pattern = Chem.MolFromSmarts(smarts)
                    if pattern and mol.HasSubstructMatch(pattern):
                        scaffold_bonus = 0.15
                        break

        # Scoring heuristic: drug-like MW (200-600) and LogP (0-5) are ideal
        mw_score = max(0.0, 1.0 - abs(mw - 400) / 400)
        logp_score = max(0.0, 1.0 - abs(log_p - 2.5) / 5.0)

        probability = min(
            1.0,
            max(0.0, 0.3 * mw_score + 0.3 * logp_score + scaffold_bonus + base_noise),
        )
        # IC50 inversely related to probability: higher probability -> lower IC50
        affinity_value = round(2.0 - 3.0 * probability, 2)  # range ~ -1 to 2

        return BindingResult(
            affinity_probability=round(probability, 4),
            affinity_value=affinity_value,
            confidence_score=round(0.3 + scaffold_bonus, 2),
            structure_path=None,
        )

--- END OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\drug_screening\boltz2_client.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\drug_screening\molmim_generator.py ---
"""MolMIM-powered novel molecule generation for AMR drug candidates.

Uses NVIDIA NIM MolMIM to generate optimized novel molecules from
seed compounds (screening hits), then validates them back through
Boltz-2 for binding confirmation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional

from src.models.nim_client import NIMClient, Boltz2Result, MolMIMResult
from src.utils.chemistry import (
    compute_descriptors,
    passes_lipinski,
    tanimoto_similarity,
    validate_smiles,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass
class NovelCandidate:
    """A novel AI-generated drug candidate."""

    smiles: str
    qed_score: float = 0.0
    seed_smiles: str = ""
    seed_drug_name: str = ""
    similarity_to_seed: float = 0.0
    passes_lipinski: bool = False
    binding_confidence: float = 0.0
    binding_iptm: float = 0.0
    binding_plddt: float = 0.0
    descriptors: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    validated: bool = False


@dataclass
class GenerationResults:
    """Results from a molecule generation + validation run."""

    seed_drug: str = ""
    seed_smiles: str = ""
    target_name: str = ""
    candidates: list[NovelCandidate] = field(default_factory=list)
    total_generated: int = 0
    total_valid: int = 0
    total_validated_binding: int = 0


class MolMIMGenerator:
    """Generates and validates novel drug candidates using MolMIM + Boltz-2."""

    def __init__(self, nim_client: Optional[NIMClient] = None) -> None:
        self._nim = nim_client or NIMClient()

    def generate_from_hit(
        self,
        seed_smiles: str,
        seed_drug_name: str = "Unknown",
        target_sequence: str = "",
        target_name: str = "",
        num_molecules: int = 5,
        property_name: str = "QED",
        min_similarity: float = 0.3,
        validate_binding: bool = True,
    ) -> GenerationResults:
        """Generate novel molecules from a screening hit and validate.

        Pipeline:
        1. MolMIM generates optimized variants of the seed
        2. Filter by Lipinski and SMILES validity
        3. (Optional) Boltz-2 validates binding to target protein

        Args:
            seed_smiles: SMILES of the seed hit compound.
            seed_drug_name: Name of the seed drug.
            target_sequence: Protein sequence for binding validation.
            target_name: Human-readable target name.
            num_molecules: Number of molecules to generate.
            property_name: Property to optimize (QED, logP, etc.).
            min_similarity: Minimum similarity to seed.
            validate_binding: Whether to run Boltz-2 binding validation.

        Returns:
            GenerationResults with validated novel candidates.
        """
        results = GenerationResults(
            seed_drug=seed_drug_name,
            seed_smiles=seed_smiles,
            target_name=target_name,
        )

        if not self._nim.available:
            logger.warning("NIM client unavailable; skipping molecule generation")
            return results

        # Step 1: Generate with MolMIM
        logger.info(
            "Generating %d novel molecules from %s (opt=%s, min_sim=%.2f)",
            num_molecules, seed_drug_name, property_name, min_similarity,
        )
        gen_result: MolMIMResult = self._nim.generate_molecules(
            seed_smiles=seed_smiles,
            num_molecules=num_molecules,
            property_name=property_name,
            min_similarity=min_similarity,
        )

        if not gen_result.success:
            logger.warning("MolMIM generation failed for %s", seed_drug_name)
            return results

        results.total_generated = gen_result.num_generated

        # Step 2: Filter and characterize
        candidates: list[NovelCandidate] = []
        for mol_data in gen_result.molecules:
            smiles = mol_data.get("sample", "")
            score = mol_data.get("score", 0.0)

            if not smiles or not validate_smiles(smiles):
                continue

            # Compute descriptors
            desc = compute_descriptors(smiles) or {}
            lipinski = passes_lipinski(smiles)

            # Similarity to seed
            sim = tanimoto_similarity(seed_smiles, smiles)
            if sim is None:
                sim = 0.0

            candidate = NovelCandidate(
                smiles=smiles,
                qed_score=round(score, 4),
                seed_smiles=seed_smiles,
                seed_drug_name=seed_drug_name,
                similarity_to_seed=round(sim, 4),
                passes_lipinski=lipinski,
                descriptors=desc,
            )
            candidates.append(candidate)

        results.total_valid = len(candidates)
        logger.info(
            "Generated %d molecules, %d valid (Lipinski pass: %d)",
            results.total_generated,
            results.total_valid,
            sum(1 for c in candidates if c.passes_lipinski),
        )

        # Step 3: Validate binding with Boltz-2
        if validate_binding and target_sequence and candidates:
            validated = 0
            for candidate in candidates:
                if not candidate.passes_lipinski:
                    continue
                try:
                    binding: Boltz2Result = self._nim.predict_binding(
                        protein_sequence=target_sequence,
                        ligand_smiles=candidate.smiles,
                    )
                    if binding.success:
                        candidate.binding_confidence = round(binding.confidence_score, 4)
                        candidate.binding_iptm = round(binding.iptm_score, 4)
                        candidate.binding_plddt = round(binding.complex_plddt, 4)
                        candidate.validated = True
                        validated += 1
                        logger.info(
                            "  Novel mol validated: conf=%.3f iptm=%.3f plddt=%.3f",
                            binding.confidence_score,
                            binding.iptm_score,
                            binding.complex_plddt,
                        )
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.warning("Boltz-2 validation failed for novel mol: %s", exc)

            results.total_validated_binding = validated

        # Sort by QED score, validated first
        candidates.sort(
            key=lambda c: (c.validated, c.qed_score), reverse=True
        )
        results.candidates = candidates
        return results

    def generate_from_top_hits(
        self,
        hits: list[dict],
        target_sequence: str = "",
        target_name: str = "",
        molecules_per_hit: int = 3,
        max_hits: int = 3,
        validate_binding: bool = True,
    ) -> list[GenerationResults]:
        """Generate novel molecules from multiple screening hits.

        Args:
            hits: List of dicts with 'smiles', 'name' keys.
            target_sequence: Protein sequence for validation.
            target_name: Human-readable target name.
            molecules_per_hit: Molecules to generate per hit.
            max_hits: Maximum hits to use as seeds.
            validate_binding: Whether to run Boltz-2 validation.

        Returns:
            List of GenerationResults, one per seed hit.
        """
        all_results: list[GenerationResults] = []
        for hit in hits[:max_hits]:
            smiles = hit.get("smiles", "")
            name = hit.get("name", hit.get("drug_name", "Unknown"))
            if not smiles:
                continue

            result = self.generate_from_hit(
                seed_smiles=smiles,
                seed_drug_name=name,
                target_sequence=target_sequence,
                target_name=target_name,
                num_molecules=molecules_per_hit,
                validate_binding=validate_binding,
            )
            all_results.append(result)

        return all_results

--- END OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\drug_screening\molmim_generator.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\drug_screening\screener.py ---
"""Drug screening orchestrator for AMR-AI platform.

Coordinates Lipinski filtering, Boltz-2 binding prediction, and
hit ranking to produce a prioritised list of drug candidates.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Generator, Optional

from src.models.nim_client import NIMClient
from src.utils.chemistry import passes_lipinski

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Data classes
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class ScreeningHit:
    """A single drug candidate that passed screening."""

    drug_name: str
    smiles: str
    chembl_id: str
    affinity_probability: float
    affinity_value: float
    rank: int = 0


@dataclass
class ScreeningResults:
    """Container for a full screening run."""

    target_name: str
    total_screened: int
    hits: list[ScreeningHit] = field(default_factory=list)
    timestamp: str = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
    )


@dataclass
class ProgressUpdate:
    """Progress update yielded during screening."""

    stage: str
    current: int
    total: int
    message: str


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Orchestrator
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class DrugScreener:
    """Orchestrates the virtual drug screening pipeline."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        nim_client: Optional[NIMClient] = None,
        binding_threshold: float = 0.5,
    ) -> None:
        self._nim = nim_client or NIMClient()
        self._binding_threshold = binding_threshold

    # -- public API ---------------------------------------------------------

    def screen_drugs(
        self,
        target_sequence: str,
        target_name: str,
        drug_library: list[dict],
        max_compounds: int = 100,
    ) -> Generator[ProgressUpdate | ScreeningResults, None, None]:
        """Screen a drug library against a protein target.

        Yields ``ProgressUpdate`` instances for UI feedback and finally
        yields the ``ScreeningResults`` as the last item.

        Args:
            target_sequence: Amino-acid sequence of the protein target.
            target_name: Human-readable target name (e.g. ``"NDM-1"``).
            drug_library: List of dicts with keys ``name``, ``smiles``,
                and optionally ``chembl_id``.
            max_compounds: Cap on the number of compounds to evaluate.
        """
        # 1. Lipinski filtering
        yield ProgressUpdate("filter", 0, len(drug_library), "Applying Lipinski filter")

        lipinski_pass: list[dict] = []
        for idx, drug in enumerate(drug_library):
            smiles = drug.get("smiles", "")
            if smiles and passes_lipinski(smiles):
                lipinski_pass.append(drug)
            if len(lipinski_pass) >= max_compounds:
                break

        yield ProgressUpdate(
            "filter", len(drug_library), len(drug_library),
            f"{len(lipinski_pass)} of {len(drug_library)} passed Lipinski",
        )

        # 2. Binding prediction
        total = len(lipinski_pass)
        hits: list[ScreeningHit] = []
        for idx, drug in enumerate(lipinski_pass):
            smiles = drug.get("smiles", "")
            yield ProgressUpdate(
                "binding", idx + 1, total,
                f"Predicting binding for {drug.get('name', smiles[:20])}",
            )

            result = self._nim.predict_binding(
                protein_sequence=target_sequence,
                ligand_smiles=smiles,
            )

            # NIMClient.predict_binding returns a Boltz2Result with
            # confidence_score (0-1) and complex_plddt as affinity proxy.
            affinity_prob = result.confidence_score if result.success else 0.0
            affinity_val = result.complex_plddt if result.success else 0.0

            if affinity_prob >= self._binding_threshold:
                hits.append(
                    ScreeningHit(
                        drug_name=drug.get("name", "Unknown"),
                        smiles=smiles,
                        chembl_id=drug.get("chembl_id", ""),
                        affinity_probability=affinity_prob,
                        affinity_value=affinity_val,
                    ),
                )

        # 3. Sort and rank
        hits.sort(key=lambda h: h.affinity_probability, reverse=True)
        for rank, hit in enumerate(hits, start=1):
            hit.rank = rank

        yield ProgressUpdate(
            "complete", total, total,
            f"Screening complete: {len(hits)} hits from {total} compounds",
        )

        # 4. Final results
        yield ScreeningResults(
            target_name=target_name,
            total_screened=total,
            hits=hits,
        )

    # -- demo mode ----------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def demo_results() -> ScreeningResults:
        """Return pre-computed demo screening results for NDM-1.

        Uses 20 known antibiotics with realistic mock affinity scores
        for demonstration and testing.
        """
        demo_hits: list[ScreeningHit] = [
            ScreeningHit("Meropenem", "C1C(C(=O)O)N2C(=O)C(C2S1(=O)=O)C(C)O", "CHEMBL127", 0.92, -1.76, 1),
            ScreeningHit("Ceftazidime", "CC(=NOC(C)(C)C(=O)O)c1csc(N)n1", "CHEMBL401", 0.88, -1.64, 2),
            ScreeningHit("Aztreonam", "CC1C(=O)N1S(=O)(=O)O", "CHEMBL158", 0.85, -1.55, 3),
            ScreeningHit("Imipenem", "C1=CN=C(N1)SCCC(=O)O", "CHEMBL560", 0.83, -1.49, 4),
            ScreeningHit("Doripenem", "CC(O)C1C2CC(=O)N2C1C(=O)O", "CHEMBL1628", 0.81, -1.43, 5),
            ScreeningHit("Cefepime", "CO/N=C(\\c1csc(N)n1)C(=O)O", "CHEMBL44", 0.78, -1.34, 6),
            ScreeningHit("Ampicillin", "CC1(C)SC2C(NC(=O)C(N)c3ccccc3)C(=O)N2C1C(=O)O", "CHEMBL174", 0.76, -1.28, 7),
            ScreeningHit("Ciprofloxacin", "O=C(O)c1cn(C2CC2)c2cc(N3CCNCC3)c(F)cc2c1=O", "CHEMBL8", 0.74, -1.22, 8),
            ScreeningHit("Levofloxacin", "CC1COc2c(N3CCN(C)CC3)c(F)cc3c(=O)c(C(=O)O)cn1c23", "CHEMBL33", 0.71, -1.13, 9),
            ScreeningHit("Amoxicillin", "CC1(C)SC2C(NC(=O)C(N)c3ccc(O)cc3)C(=O)N2C1C(=O)O", "CHEMBL1082", 0.69, -1.07, 10),
            ScreeningHit("Piperacillin", "CC1(C)SC2C(NC(=O)C(NC(=O)N3CCNC3=O)c3ccccc3)C(=O)N2C1C(=O)O", "CHEMBL484", 0.66, -0.98, 11),
            ScreeningHit("Ertapenem", "CC(O)C1C(=O)N2C(C(=O)O)=C(SC3CNC(=O)C3)CC12", "CHEMBL1160", 0.64, -0.92, 12),
            ScreeningHit("Cefotaxime", "CO/N=C(\\c1csc(N)n1)C(=O)NC1C(=O)N2C1SCC2=C", "CHEMBL161", 0.61, -0.83, 13),
            ScreeningHit("Tigecycline", "CN(C)c1cc(NC(=O)CNC(C)(C)C)c(O)c2c1CC1CC3C(N(C)C)C(O)=C(C(=O)N)C(=O)C3(O)C(O)=C1C2=O", "CHEMBL376", 0.58, -0.74, 14),
            ScreeningHit("Colistin", "CCCCCC(CC(=O)NC(CCN)C(=O)O)OC(=O)C(CC(=O)O)NC=O", "CHEMBL1489", 0.56, -0.68, 15),
            ScreeningHit("Fosfomycin", "CC1OC1P(=O)(O)O", "CHEMBL374", 0.54, -0.62, 16),
            ScreeningHit("Trimethoprim", "COc1cc(Cc2cnc(N)nc2N)cc(OC)c1OC", "CHEMBL22", 0.52, -0.56, 17),
            ScreeningHit("Sulfamethoxazole", "Cc1cc(NS(=O)(=O)c2ccc(N)cc2)no1", "CHEMBL21", 0.51, -0.53, 18),
            ScreeningHit("Vancomycin", "CC(O)C(NC(=O)C1CC(=O)NC1)C(=O)O", "CHEMBL262777", 0.50, -0.50, 19),
            ScreeningHit("Rifampicin", "CC1C=CC=C(C)C(=O)NC(=CC=CC(C)C(O)C(C)C(=O)C(C)C(OC(=O)C)C1O)C", "CHEMBL374478", 0.48, -0.44, 20),
        ]

        return ScreeningResults(
            target_name="NDM-1 (Metallo-beta-lactamase)",
            total_screened=20,
            hits=[h for h in demo_hits if h.affinity_probability >= 0.5],
        )

--- END OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\drug_screening\screener.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\drug_screening\similarity_search.py ---
"""Chemical similarity search and clustering for AMR-AI drug screening.

Uses Tanimoto similarity on Morgan fingerprints (via RDKit) to find
structurally related drugs and group screening hits into clusters.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Optional

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

from src.utils.chemistry import (
    RDKIT_AVAILABLE,
    tanimoto_similarity,
)

try:
    from rdkit import Chem, DataStructs
    from rdkit.Chem import AllChem
    from rdkit.ML.Cluster import Butina
except ImportError:
    pass  # RDKIT_AVAILABLE already False from chemistry module


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fingerprint helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _morgan_fp(smiles: str, radius: int = 2, n_bits: int = 2048) -> Optional[object]:
    """Compute a Morgan fingerprint bit vector for a SMILES string."""
    if not RDKIT_AVAILABLE:
        return None
    mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)
    if mol is None:
        return None
    return AllChem.GetMorganFingerprintAsBitVect(mol, radius, nBits=n_bits)


def _tanimoto(fp1: object, fp2: object) -> float:
    """Return the Tanimoto similarity between two fingerprints."""
    if fp1 is None or fp2 is None:
        return 0.0
    return float(DataStructs.TanimotoSimilarity(fp1, fp2))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Similarity search
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def find_similar_drugs(
    query_smiles: str,
    drug_library: list[dict],
    top_k: int = 20,
) -> list[dict]:
    """Find the most structurally similar drugs to a query molecule.

    Args:
        query_smiles: SMILES string for the query compound.
        drug_library: List of dicts, each with at least a ``smiles`` key.
            May also contain ``name``, ``chembl_id``, etc.
        top_k: Number of top similar drugs to return.

    Returns:
        List of drug dicts augmented with a ``similarity`` score,
        sorted descending by similarity.
    """
    if not RDKIT_AVAILABLE:
        logger.warning("RDKit unavailable; returning first %d drugs unsorted", top_k)
        results: list[dict] = []
        for drug in drug_library[:top_k]:
            entry = dict(drug)
            entry["similarity"] = 0.0
            results.append(entry)
        return results

    query_fp = _morgan_fp(query_smiles)
    if query_fp is None:
        logger.error("Invalid query SMILES: %s", query_smiles)
        return []

    scored: list[tuple[float, dict]] = []
    for drug in drug_library:
        smiles = drug.get("smiles", "")
        if not smiles:
            continue
        drug_fp = _morgan_fp(smiles)
        if drug_fp is None:
            continue
        sim = _tanimoto(query_fp, drug_fp)
        scored.append((sim, drug))

    scored.sort(key=lambda t: t[0], reverse=True)

    results = []
    for sim, drug in scored[:top_k]:
        entry = dict(drug)
        entry["similarity"] = round(sim, 4)
        results.append(entry)
    return results


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Clustering
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def cluster_hits(
    hits: list[dict],
    threshold: float = 0.7,
) -> list[list[dict]]:
    """Group hits into clusters based on Tanimoto similarity.

    Uses Butina clustering (Taylor-Butina algorithm) when RDKit is
    available.  Falls back to single-element clusters otherwise.

    Args:
        hits: List of dicts, each with a ``smiles`` key.
        threshold: Tanimoto distance cutoff (1 - similarity).
            Default 0.7 means molecules with >=0.3 similarity cluster.

    Returns:
        List of clusters, each cluster a list of drug dicts.
        Clusters are sorted largest-first.
    """
    if not hits:
        return []

    if not RDKIT_AVAILABLE:
        return [[h] for h in hits]

    # Build fingerprints, skipping invalid SMILES
    fps: list[object] = []
    valid_hits: list[dict] = []
    for hit in hits:
        fp = _morgan_fp(hit.get("smiles", ""))
        if fp is not None:
            fps.append(fp)
            valid_hits.append(hit)

    if not fps:
        return [[h] for h in hits]

    # Compute pairwise distance matrix (upper triangle)
    n = len(fps)
    dists: list[float] = []
    for i in range(1, n):
        for j in range(i):
            dists.append(1.0 - _tanimoto(fps[i], fps[j]))

    # Butina clustering
    clusters_idx = Butina.ClusterData(dists, n, threshold, isDistData=True)

    clusters: list[list[dict]] = []
    for idx_tuple in clusters_idx:
        cluster = [valid_hits[i] for i in idx_tuple]
        clusters.append(cluster)

    # Sort clusters largest-first
    clusters.sort(key=len, reverse=True)
    return clusters

--- START OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\safety_prediction\admet_predictor.py ---
"""ADMET property prediction with heuristic fallback using RDKit descriptors."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# SMARTS patterns for common toxicophores
TOXICOPHORE_SMARTS: dict[str, str] = {
    "nitro_aromatic": "[$(c1ccccc1[N+](=O)[O-])]",
    "aniline": "[NH2]c1ccccc1",
    "michael_acceptor": "[#6]=[#6]-[#6]=[O,N,S]",
    "epoxide": "C1OC1",
    "aldehyde": "[CH]=O",
    "acyl_halide": "[#6](=O)[F,Cl,Br,I]",
    "sulfonyl_halide": "S(=O)(=O)[F,Cl,Br,I]",
    "polycyclic_aromatic": "c1ccc2c(c1)ccc1ccccc12",
    "hydrazine": "[NH]-[NH]",
    "thiocarbonyl": "[#6](=S)",
}


@dataclass
class ADMETResult:
    """Full ADMET property prediction result."""

    absorption: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    distribution: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    metabolism: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    excretion: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    toxicity: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    overall_safety_score: float = 0.5


def _compute_rdkit_descriptors(smiles: str) -> dict:
    """Compute molecular descriptors using RDKit."""
    try:
        from rdkit import Chem
        from rdkit.Chem import Descriptors, rdMolDescriptors
    except ImportError:
        logger.warning("RDKit not available; using placeholder descriptors")
        return _placeholder_descriptors(smiles)

    mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)
    if mol is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid SMILES: {smiles}")

    return {
        "mw": Descriptors.MolWt(mol),
        "logp": Descriptors.MolLogP(mol),
        "tpsa": Descriptors.TPSA(mol),
        "hbd": rdMolDescriptors.CalcNumHBD(mol),
        "hba": rdMolDescriptors.CalcNumHBA(mol),
        "rotatable_bonds": rdMolDescriptors.CalcNumRotatableBonds(mol),
        "aromatic_rings": rdMolDescriptors.CalcNumAromaticRings(mol),
        "heavy_atoms": mol.GetNumHeavyAtoms(),
        "toxicophore_count": _count_toxicophores(mol),
        "ring_count": rdMolDescriptors.CalcNumRings(mol),
    }


def _placeholder_descriptors(smiles: str) -> dict:
    """Rough descriptor estimates when RDKit is unavailable."""
    length = len(smiles)
    return {
        "mw": length * 8.5,
        "logp": min(length * 0.08, 5.5),
        "tpsa": length * 2.0,
        "hbd": smiles.count("N") + smiles.count("O"),
        "hba": smiles.count("N") + smiles.count("O") + smiles.count("F"),
        "rotatable_bonds": max(0, length // 8),
        "aromatic_rings": smiles.lower().count("c1"),
        "heavy_atoms": length // 2,
        "toxicophore_count": 0,
        "ring_count": smiles.count("1") // 2 + smiles.count("2") // 2,
    }


def _count_toxicophores(mol) -> int:
    """Count the number of toxicophore substructure matches."""
    from rdkit import Chem

    count = 0
    for name, smarts in TOXICOPHORE_SMARTS.items():
        pattern = Chem.MolFromSmarts(smarts)
        if pattern and mol.HasSubstructMatch(pattern):
            count += 1
    return count


def _sigmoid(x: float) -> float:
    """Numerically stable sigmoid mapping."""
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-max(-500, min(500, x))))


def _heuristic_absorption(desc: dict) -> dict:
    """Predict absorption properties from descriptors."""
    logp = desc["logp"]
    tpsa = desc["tpsa"]
    mw = desc["mw"]
    hbd = desc["hbd"]
    hba = desc["hba"]

    caco2 = round(-5.5 + 0.3 * logp - 0.01 * tpsa, 3)
    hua = round(max(0, min(100, 95 - 0.5 * (tpsa - 75) - 2 * max(0, hbd - 3))), 1)
    pgp = bool(mw > 400 and hbd > 2 and tpsa > 100)

    return {
        "caco2_permeability": caco2,
        "human_intestinal_absorption": hua,
        "pgp_substrate": pgp,
    }


def _heuristic_distribution(desc: dict) -> dict:
    """Predict distribution properties."""
    logp = desc["logp"]
    mw = desc["mw"]
    tpsa = desc["tpsa"]

    vdss = round(max(0.04, 0.04 + 0.15 * logp - 0.001 * tpsa), 3)
    bbb = round(max(0, min(1, _sigmoid(0.6 * logp - 0.02 * tpsa - 0.002 * mw))), 3)
    ppb = round(min(99.9, max(10, 50 + 10 * logp + 0.05 * mw)), 1)

    return {
        "vdss": vdss,
        "bbb_penetration": bbb,
        "plasma_protein_binding": ppb,
    }


def _heuristic_metabolism(desc: dict) -> dict:
    """Predict CYP inhibition liability."""
    logp = desc["logp"]
    ar = desc["aromatic_rings"]
    mw = desc["mw"]

    base_risk = 0.1 + 0.08 * logp + 0.05 * ar
    return {
        "cyp1a2_inhibitor": round(min(1, base_risk + 0.02 * ar), 3),
        "cyp2c9_inhibitor": round(min(1, base_risk + 0.01 * (mw / 100)), 3),
        "cyp2c19_inhibitor": round(min(1, base_risk), 3),
        "cyp2d6_inhibitor": round(min(1, base_risk + 0.03 * logp), 3),
        "cyp3a4_inhibitor": round(min(1, base_risk + 0.04 * ar), 3),
    }


def _heuristic_excretion(desc: dict) -> dict:
    """Predict excretion properties."""
    mw = desc["mw"]
    logp = desc["logp"]

    clearance = round(max(0.1, 1.2 - 0.001 * mw + 0.05 * logp), 3)
    oct2 = bool(logp < 1 and mw < 400)

    return {"total_clearance": clearance, "renal_oct2_substrate": oct2}


def _heuristic_toxicity(desc: dict) -> dict:
    """Predict toxicity endpoints from descriptors."""
    logp = desc["logp"]
    mw = desc["mw"]
    tpsa = desc["tpsa"]
    tc = desc["toxicophore_count"]

    herg = round(min(1, 0.05 + 0.1 * max(0, logp - 3) + 0.02 * tc), 3)
    ames = round(min(1, 0.05 + 0.15 * tc), 3)
    hepato = round(min(1, 0.1 + 0.05 * max(0, logp - 3) + 0.1 * tc), 3)
    skin = round(min(1, 0.05 + 0.08 * tc + 0.02 * max(0, logp - 2)), 3)
    ld50 = round(max(50, 2500 - 100 * tc - 50 * max(0, logp - 3) - 0.5 * mw), 1)

    return {
        "herg_inhibition": herg,
        "ames_mutagenicity": ames,
        "hepatotoxicity": hepato,
        "skin_sensitization": skin,
        "oral_rat_ld50": ld50,
    }


def _calculate_safety_score(admet: ADMETResult) -> float:
    """Compute an overall safety score (0-1, 1 = safest)."""
    tox = admet.toxicity
    penalties = 0.0
    penalties += tox.get("herg_inhibition", 0) * 0.3
    penalties += tox.get("ames_mutagenicity", 0) * 0.25
    penalties += tox.get("hepatotoxicity", 0) * 0.25
    penalties += tox.get("skin_sensitization", 0) * 0.1

    ld50 = tox.get("oral_rat_ld50", 2000)
    if ld50 < 300:
        penalties += 0.1

    abs_score = min(1, admet.absorption.get("human_intestinal_absorption", 80) / 100)
    score = max(0, min(1, 1.0 - penalties)) * 0.7 + abs_score * 0.3
    return round(max(0, min(1, score)), 3)


class ADMETPredictor:
    """ADMET property predictor with API integration and heuristic fallback."""

    def __init__(self, api_url: str | None = None, api_key: str | None = None) -> None:
        self._api_url = api_url
        self._api_key = api_key
        self._api_available = False
        if api_url and api_key:
            self._api_available = True
            logger.info("ADMET API configured: %s", api_url)
        else:
            logger.info("No ADMET API configured; using heuristic fallback")

    def predict_admet(self, smiles: str) -> ADMETResult:
        """Predict ADMET properties for a compound.

        Args:
            smiles: SMILES string of the molecule.

        Returns:
            ADMETResult with all ADMET property predictions.
        """
        if not smiles or not smiles.strip():
            raise ValueError("SMILES string cannot be empty")

        if self._api_available:
            try:
                return self._predict_via_api(smiles)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.warning("API prediction failed: %s; using fallback", exc)

        return self._predict_heuristic(smiles)

    def _predict_via_api(self, smiles: str) -> ADMETResult:
        """Attempt prediction via external API (pkCSM or ADMETlab)."""
        import urllib.request
        import json

        payload = json.dumps({"smiles": smiles}).encode()
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            self._api_url,
            data=payload,
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": f"Bearer {self._api_key}"},
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as resp:
            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())

        return ADMETResult(
            absorption=data.get("absorption", {}),
            distribution=data.get("distribution", {}),
            metabolism=data.get("metabolism", {}),
            excretion=data.get("excretion", {}),
            toxicity=data.get("toxicity", {}),
            overall_safety_score=data.get("overall_safety_score", 0.5),
        )

    def _predict_heuristic(self, smiles: str) -> ADMETResult:
        """Predict ADMET using RDKit descriptors and rule-based heuristics."""
        desc = _compute_rdkit_descriptors(smiles)

        result = ADMETResult(
            absorption=_heuristic_absorption(desc),
            distribution=_heuristic_distribution(desc),
            metabolism=_heuristic_metabolism(desc),
            excretion=_heuristic_excretion(desc),
            toxicity=_heuristic_toxicity(desc),
        )
        result.overall_safety_score = _calculate_safety_score(result)
        return result

--- END OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\safety_prediction\admet_predictor.py ---

@dataclass
class OffTarget:
    """A single off-target binding prediction."""

    protein_name: str
    similarity_score: float
    tissue_expression: str
    known_adverse_effects: str


@dataclass
class OffTargetReport:
    """Complete off-target analysis report."""

    drug_name: str
    num_potential_off_targets: int = 0
    off_targets: list[OffTarget] = field(default_factory=list)
    risk_level: str = "low"


def _compute_tanimoto(smiles_a: str, smiles_b: str) -> float:
    """Compute Tanimoto similarity between two molecules."""
    try:
        from rdkit import Chem
        from rdkit.Chem import AllChem, DataStructs

        mol_a = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles_a)
        mol_b = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles_b)
        if mol_a is None or mol_b is None:
            return 0.0
        fp_a = AllChem.GetMorganFingerprintAsBitVect(mol_a, 2, nBits=2048)
        fp_b = AllChem.GetMorganFingerprintAsBitVect(mol_b, 2, nBits=2048)
        return round(DataStructs.TanimotoSimilarity(fp_a, fp_b), 4)
    except ImportError:
        return _fallback_similarity(smiles_a, smiles_b)


def _fallback_similarity(a: str, b: str) -> float:
    """Simple character-set Jaccard when RDKit is unavailable."""
    set_a = set(a)
    set_b = set(b)
    if not set_a or not set_b:
        return 0.0
    return round(len(set_a & set_b) / len(set_a | set_b), 4)


def _classify_risk(hits: list[OffTarget]) -> str:
    """Classify overall risk from off-target hits."""
    if not hits:
        return "low"
    max_sim = max(h.similarity_score for h in hits)
    cardiac_hit = any("heart" in h.tissue_expression.lower() or "cardiac" in h.known_adverse_effects.lower() for h in hits)
    if max_sim > 0.6 or (cardiac_hit and max_sim > 0.4):
        return "high"
    if max_sim > 0.35 or len(hits) >= 5:
        return "medium"
    return "low"


class OffTargetAnalyzer:
    """Analyzes potential off-target binding liabilities of a drug candidate."""

    def __init__(self, similarity_threshold: float = 0.25) -> None:
        self._threshold = similarity_threshold

    def analyze_off_targets(
        self, drug_smiles: str, target_sequence: str = ""
    ) -> OffTargetReport:
        """Scan drug against curated anti-target panel.

        Args:
            drug_smiles: SMILES of the drug candidate.
            target_sequence: Primary target protein sequence (informational).

        Returns:
            OffTargetReport with flagged off-target liabilities.
        """
        if not drug_smiles or not drug_smiles.strip():
            raise ValueError("Drug SMILES cannot be empty")

        hits: list[OffTarget] = []
        for entry in ANTI_TARGETS:
            sim = _compute_tanimoto(drug_smiles, entry["binder_smiles"])
            if sim >= self._threshold:
                hits.append(OffTarget(
                    protein_name=entry["protein"],
                    similarity_score=sim,
                    tissue_expression=entry["tissue"],
                    known_adverse_effects=entry["adverse"],
                ))

        hits.sort(key=lambda h: h.similarity_score, reverse=True)
        risk = _classify_risk(hits)

        return OffTargetReport(
            drug_name=drug_smiles[:40],
            num_potential_off_targets=len(hits),
            off_targets=hits,
            risk_level=risk,
        )

--- END OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\safety_prediction\off_target_analyzer.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\safety_prediction\safety_scorer.py ---
"""Combined safety scoring that merges ADMET and off-target analyses."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from .admet_predictor import ADMETPredictor, ADMETResult
from .off_target_analyzer import OffTargetAnalyzer, OffTargetReport

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Pre-computed demo data for common antibiotics (SMILES sourced from PubChem).
DEMO_DRUGS: dict[str, dict] = {
    "ciprofloxacin": {
        "smiles": "O=C(O)c1cn(C2CC2)c2cc(N3CCNCC3)c(F)cc2c1=O",
        "name": "Ciprofloxacin",
    },
    "amoxicillin": {
        "smiles": "CC1(C)SC2C(NC(=O)C(N)c3ccc(O)cc3)C(=O)N2C1C(=O)O",
        "name": "Amoxicillin",
    },
    "doxycycline": {
        "smiles": "OC1C2CC3C(=C(O)c4c(O)cccc4C3(C)O)C(=O)C2(O)CC1N(C)C",
        "name": "Doxycycline",
    },
    "azithromycin": {
        "smiles": "CCC1OC(=O)C(C)C(OC2CC(C)(OC)C(O)C(C)O2)C(C)C(OC2OC(C)CC(N(C)C)C2O)C(C)(O)CC(C)CN(C)C1C",
        "name": "Azithromycin",
    },
    "trimethoprim": {
        "smiles": "COc1cc(Cc2cnc(N)nc2N)cc(OC)c1OC",
        "name": "Trimethoprim",
    },
}


@dataclass
class SafetyReport:
    """Complete safety assessment for a drug candidate."""

    drug_name: str
    smiles: str
    admet: ADMETResult | None = None
    off_targets: OffTargetReport | None = None
    overall_score: float = 0.0
    risk_level: str = "unknown"
    regulatory_flags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    recommendation: str = ""


def _generate_regulatory_flags(
    admet: ADMETResult, off_target: OffTargetReport
) -> list[str]:
    """Identify regulatory red flags from ADMET and off-target data."""
    flags: list[str] = []

    tox = admet.toxicity
    if tox.get("herg_inhibition", 0) > 0.4:
        flags.append("hERG liability detected (cardiac risk)")
    if tox.get("ames_mutagenicity", 0) > 0.5:
        flags.append("Ames mutagenicity concern")
    if tox.get("hepatotoxicity", 0) > 0.5:
        flags.append("Hepatotoxicity signal")
    if tox.get("oral_rat_ld50", 2000) < 300:
        flags.append("Acute oral toxicity (LD50 < 300 mg/kg)")

    metab = admet.metabolism
    cyp_inhibitors = [k for k, v in metab.items() if v > 0.5]
    if cyp_inhibitors:
        names = ", ".join(k.replace("_inhibitor", "").upper() for k in cyp_inhibitors)
        flags.append(f"CYP inhibition: {names}")

    if admet.distribution.get("bbb_penetration", 0) > 0.7:
        flags.append("High BBB penetration (CNS exposure)")

    for hit in off_target.off_targets:
        if hit.similarity_score > 0.5:
            flags.append(
                f"Off-target: {hit.protein_name} "
                f"(sim={hit.similarity_score:.2f}, {hit.known_adverse_effects})"
            )

    return flags


def _classify_risk(score: float) -> str:
    """Map numeric score to risk category."""
    if score >= 75:
        return "low"
    if score >= 50:
        return "medium"
    return "high"


def _generate_recommendation(
    score: float, risk: str, flags: list[str]
) -> str:
    """Generate human-readable recommendation text."""
    if risk == "low" and not flags:
        return "Compound shows favorable safety profile. Recommend proceeding to lead optimization."

    if risk == "low":
        flag_text = "; ".join(flags[:3])
        return (
            f"Generally favorable safety profile with minor flags: {flag_text}. "
            "Consider targeted assays to confirm."
        )

    if risk == "medium":
        flag_text = "; ".join(flags[:3])
        return (
            f"Moderate safety concerns identified: {flag_text}. "
            "Recommend structural optimization to mitigate liabilities before advancing."
        )

    flag_text = "; ".join(flags[:3])
    return (
        f"Significant safety liabilities detected: {flag_text}. "
        "Recommend deprioritization or major structural redesign."
    )


class SafetyScorer:
    """Orchestrates ADMET and off-target analysis into a unified safety score."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        admet_api_url: str | None = None,
        admet_api_key: str | None = None,
        similarity_threshold: float = 0.25,
    ) -> None:
        self._admet = ADMETPredictor(api_url=admet_api_url, api_key=admet_api_key)
        self._off_target = OffTargetAnalyzer(similarity_threshold=similarity_threshold)

    def score_drug(
        self,
        smiles: str,
        drug_name: str,
        target_sequence: str | None = None,
    ) -> SafetyReport:
        """Run full safety assessment on a single compound.

        Args:
            smiles: SMILES string of the drug candidate.
            drug_name: Human-readable name.
            target_sequence: Optional primary target protein sequence.

        Returns:
            SafetyReport with combined scoring and regulatory flags.
        """
        if not smiles or not smiles.strip():
            raise ValueError("SMILES string cannot be empty")

        admet_result = self._admet.predict_admet(smiles)
        off_target_result = self._off_target.analyze_off_targets(
            smiles, target_sequence or ""
        )

        # Weighted combination: ADMET 60%, off-target 40%
        admet_score = admet_result.overall_safety_score * 100
        ot_hits = off_target_result.num_potential_off_targets
        max_sim = max(
            (h.similarity_score for h in off_target_result.off_targets), default=0
        )
        ot_score = max(0, 100 - ot_hits * 8 - max_sim * 40)

        combined = round(admet_score * 0.6 + ot_score * 0.4, 1)
        combined = max(0, min(100, combined))

        risk = _classify_risk(combined)
        flags = _generate_regulatory_flags(admet_result, off_target_result)
        recommendation = _generate_recommendation(combined, risk, flags)

        return SafetyReport(
            drug_name=drug_name,
            smiles=smiles,
            admet=admet_result,
            off_targets=off_target_result,
            overall_score=combined,
            risk_level=risk,
            regulatory_flags=flags,
            recommendation=recommendation,
        )

    def batch_score(self, drug_list: list[dict]) -> list[SafetyReport]:
        """Score multiple drugs.

        Args:
            drug_list: List of dicts with keys 'smiles', 'name', and optional
                       'target_sequence'.

        Returns:
            List of SafetyReport objects.
        """
        results: list[SafetyReport] = []
        for entry in drug_list:
            smiles = entry.get("smiles", "")
            name = entry.get("name", "unknown")
            target = entry.get("target_sequence")
            try:
                report = self.score_drug(smiles, name, target)
                results.append(report)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error("Failed to score %s: %s", name, exc)
                results.append(SafetyReport(
                    drug_name=name,
                    smiles=smiles,
                    risk_level="error",
                    recommendation=f"Scoring failed: {exc}",
                ))
        return results

    def score_demo_antibiotics(self) -> list[SafetyReport]:
        """Run safety scoring on built-in demo antibiotic set.

        Returns:
            List of SafetyReport for common antibiotics.
        """
        drug_list = [
            {"smiles": d["smiles"], "name": d["name"]}
            for d in DEMO_DRUGS.values()
        ]
        return self.batch_score(drug_list)

"""ESM-2 protein analysis client for embeddings and druggability prediction."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Any

import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

ESM2_MODEL_NAME = "facebook/esm2_t33_650M_UR50D"
EMBEDDING_DIM = 1280


def _mock_embedding(seq_len: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Generate a deterministic mock embedding with realistic shape."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed=hash(str(seq_len)) % (2**31))
    return rng.standard_normal((seq_len, EMBEDDING_DIM)).astype(np.float32)


class ESM2Client:
    """Client for ESM-2 protein language model embeddings and analysis."""

    def __init__(self, model_name: str = ESM2_MODEL_NAME) -> None:
        self._model_name = model_name
        self._tokenizer = None
        self._model = None
        self._available = False
        self._device = "cpu"
        self._init_model()

    def _init_model(self) -> None:
        """Load ESM-2 model and tokenizer from HuggingFace."""
        try:
            import torch
            from transformers import AutoModel, AutoTokenizer

            self._tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(self._model_name)
            self._model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(self._model_name)

            if torch.cuda.is_available():
                self._device = "cuda"
                self._model = self._model.to(self._device)
                logger.info("ESM-2 model loaded on GPU")
            else:
                logger.info("ESM-2 model loaded on CPU")

            self._model.eval()
            self._available = True
        except ImportError:
            logger.warning("transformers/torch not installed; ESM-2 will use mock embeddings")
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Failed to load ESM-2 model: %s; using mock embeddings", exc)

    def get_embeddings(self, protein_sequence: str) -> np.ndarray:
        """Generate per-residue ESM-2 embeddings for a protein sequence.

        Args:
            protein_sequence: Amino acid sequence string (e.g. 'MKTL...').

        Returns:
            Numpy array of shape (seq_len, 1280) with per-residue embeddings.
        """
        if not self._available:
            logger.info("Using mock embeddings for sequence of length %d", len(protein_sequence))
            return _mock_embedding(len(protein_sequence))

        try:
            import torch

            inputs = self._tokenizer(
                protein_sequence,
                return_tensors="pt",
                padding=False,
                truncation=True,
                max_length=1024,
            )
            inputs = {k: v.to(self._device) for k, v in inputs.items()}

            with torch.no_grad():
                outputs = self._model(**inputs)

            # Strip [CLS] and [EOS] tokens from last hidden state
            embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state[0, 1:-1, :].cpu().numpy()
            return embeddings
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("ESM-2 embedding failed: %s; returning mock", exc)
            return _mock_embedding(len(protein_sequence))

    def get_sequence_embedding(self, protein_sequence: str) -> np.ndarray:
        """Get a single fixed-length embedding by mean-pooling residue embeddings.

        Args:
            protein_sequence: Amino acid sequence string.

        Returns:
            Numpy array of shape (1280,) representing the whole sequence.
        """
        per_residue = self.get_embeddings(protein_sequence)
        return np.mean(per_residue, axis=0)

    def predict_druggability(self, protein_sequence: str) -> float:
        """Predict druggability score for a protein based on embedding features.

        Heuristic scoring based on:
        - Sequence length (ideal binding pockets in 200-800 residue range)
        - Embedding variance (structural complexity)
        - Hydrophobic residue fraction (binding pocket character)

        Args:
            protein_sequence: Amino acid sequence string.

        Returns:
            Float between 0.0 and 1.0 (higher = more druggable).
        """
        embeddings = self.get_embeddings(protein_sequence)
        seq_len = len(protein_sequence)

        # Length score: proteins 200-800 residues are often druggable enzymes
        if 200 <= seq_len <= 800:
            length_score = 1.0
        elif seq_len < 100 or seq_len > 2000:
            length_score = 0.3
        else:
            length_score = 0.7

        # Embedding variance as proxy for structural complexity
        variance = float(np.mean(np.var(embeddings, axis=0)))
        variance_score = min(1.0, variance / 2.0)

        # Hydrophobic residue fraction (binding pocket heuristic)
        hydrophobic = set("AILMFWVP")
        hydro_frac = sum(1 for aa in protein_sequence if aa in hydrophobic) / max(seq_len, 1)
        hydro_score = min(1.0, hydro_frac / 0.4)  # normalize around typical 30-40%

        # Weighted combination
        score = 0.35 * length_score + 0.40 * variance_score + 0.25 * hydro_score
        return round(min(1.0, max(0.0, score)), 4)

    def find_similar_proteins(
        self,
        query_embedding: np.ndarray,
        reference_embeddings: list[tuple[str, np.ndarray]],
        top_k: int = 10,
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Find most similar proteins by cosine similarity of sequence embeddings.

        Args:
            query_embedding: 1-D array of shape (1280,) for the query protein.
            reference_embeddings: List of (protein_id, embedding) tuples.
            top_k: Number of top matches to return.

        Returns:
            Sorted list of dicts with 'protein_id' and 'similarity' keys.
        """
        if len(reference_embeddings) == 0:
            return []

        query_norm = query_embedding / (np.linalg.norm(query_embedding) + 1e-10)
        results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

        for protein_id, ref_emb in reference_embeddings:
            ref_norm = ref_emb / (np.linalg.norm(ref_emb) + 1e-10)
            similarity = float(np.dot(query_norm, ref_norm))
            results.append({"protein_id": protein_id, "similarity": round(similarity, 4)})

        results.sort(key=lambda x: x["similarity"], reverse=True)
        return results[:top_k]

class Evo2Client:
    """Client for Evo2-40b genomic analysis via Nebius AI Studio."""

    def __init__(self, base_url: str = NEBIUS_BASE_URL, api_key: str | None = None) -> None:
        self._base_url = base_url
        self._api_key = api_key or os.environ.get("NEBIUS_API_KEY", "")
        self._client = None
        self._available = False
        self._init_client()

    def _init_client(self) -> None:
        """Initialize the OpenAI-compatible client for Nebius."""
        if not self._api_key:
            logger.warning("NEBIUS_API_KEY not set; Evo2 client will use demo mode")
            return
        try:
            from openai import OpenAI

            self._client = OpenAI(base_url=self._base_url, api_key=self._api_key)
            self._available = True
            logger.info("Evo2 client initialized with Nebius AI Studio")
        except ImportError:
            logger.warning("openai package not installed; Evo2 client will use demo mode")
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Failed to initialize Evo2 client: %s", exc)

    def analyze_genome(self, sequence: str) -> dict:
        """Send a genomic DNA sequence to Evo2-40b for analysis.

        Args:
            sequence: Nucleotide sequence string (A/T/G/C).

        Returns:
            Analysis results including coding regions, islands, and summary.
        """
        if not self._available or not self._client:
            logger.info("Using demo mode for genome analysis")
            return _get_mock_genome_analysis()

        try:
            prompt = (
                "Analyze this bacterial genomic DNA sequence for resistance elements, "
                "coding regions, and genomic islands. Return structured JSON.\n\n"
                f"Sequence (first 2000 bp): {sequence[:2000]}"
            )
            response = self._client.chat.completions.create(
                model=EVO2_MODEL,
                messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
                max_tokens=2048,
                temperature=0.1,
            )
            content = response.choices[0].message.content or ""
            try:
                return json.loads(content)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                return {"model": EVO2_MODEL, "raw_response": content}
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Evo2 API call failed: %s; falling back to demo data", exc)
            return _get_mock_genome_analysis()

    def identify_resistance_genes(self, sequence: str) -> list[dict]:
        """Analyze a genomic sequence for antimicrobial resistance gene signatures.

        Args:
            sequence: Nucleotide sequence string.

        Returns:
            List of detected resistance genes with metadata.
        """
        if not self._available or not self._client:
            logger.info("Using demo mode for resistance gene identification")
            return _get_mock_resistance_genes()

        try:
            prompt = (
                "Identify antimicrobial resistance genes in this DNA sequence. "
                "For each gene return: gene_name, gene_family, mechanism, drug_class, "
                "confidence (0-1), position_start, position_end. Return JSON array.\n\n"
                f"Sequence (first 2000 bp): {sequence[:2000]}"
            )
            response = self._client.chat.completions.create(
                model=EVO2_MODEL,
                messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
                max_tokens=2048,
                temperature=0.1,
            )
            content = response.choices[0].message.content or ""
            try:
                result = json.loads(content)
                return result if isinstance(result, list) else [result]
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                logger.warning("Could not parse Evo2 response as JSON; using demo data")
                return _get_mock_resistance_genes()
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Evo2 resistance gene call failed: %s; using demo data", exc)
            return _get_mock_resistance_genes()

--- START OF FILE: .\src\pipeline\target_discovery\target_analyzer.py ---
"""Target discovery orchestrator for AMR drug targets."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import yaml

from .esm2_client import ESM2Client

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "config" / "settings.yaml"
UNIPROT_REST = "https://rest.uniprot.org"


@dataclass
class TargetResult:
    """A druggable protein target identified through analysis."""

    name: str
    pdb_id: str
    uniprot_id: str
    function: str
    druggability_score: float
    resistance_mechanism: str
    confidence: float
    pathogen: str = ""
    inhibitors: str = ""
    embedding_available: bool = False


def _load_config() -> dict:
    """Load settings.yaml configuration."""
    try:
        with open(CONFIG_PATH) as f:
            return yaml.safe_load(f)
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("Failed to load config from %s: %s", CONFIG_PATH, exc)
        return {}


def _fetch_fasta(uniprot_id: str) -> str | None:
    """Fetch FASTA protein sequence from UniProt REST API.

    Args:
        uniprot_id: UniProt accession (e.g. 'C7C422').

    Returns:
        Protein sequence string or None on failure.
    """
    try:
        import requests

        url = f"{UNIPROT_REST}/uniprot/{uniprot_id}.fasta"
        resp = requests.get(url, timeout=15)
        if resp.status_code == 200:
            lines = resp.text.strip().split("\n")
            return "".join(line for line in lines if not line.startswith(">"))
        logger.warning("UniProt returned %d for %s", resp.status_code, uniprot_id)
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning("Failed to fetch FASTA for %s: %s", uniprot_id, exc)
    return None


# Pre-computed demo sequences (truncated representative sequences)
_DEMO_SEQUENCES: dict[str, str] = {
    "C7C422": "MELPNIMHPVAKLSTALAAALMLSGCSFAQTPNPVNREVNPGAAMQHWDDKISGNA",
    "Q2L4R8": "MMKKTLCALIAVSLTTAAGCCENQFNISTSNIHFAGDEVYAQFMDMVNEQGDGQWF",
    "P07944": "MKNNKIICLTAAFLILTAGCQNSDSSIEKHFDQYTKDSDIFAKAGLKKKSDVHFKEV",
    "P0A0K8": "MSDLAREITPVNIEEELKSSYLDYAMSVIVGRALPDVRDGLKPVHRRVLYAMNVLGN",
    "Q6PT52": "MRYICLLLITPLIAGCDHAKRFSEQHINEFSQNELKLETTNLLFKQEVNIKPNWREA",
    "P52477": "MNIRNIFHKHDAKYTFYIISALSLLSLVAVTFRFVVSAQESMNIQFPGDLKQNEQRF",
    "P25084": "MAEYQNIFVDHINELKTMPSHRLSARYNKQGFAGLLTPEWLAQHGISMYVGEELRQH",
    "A0A0D5YPF0": "MKKHSLTTLALFISATFAQADSQKNAGEKRFAPVAEKMKSTGKDVF",
}


def _get_demo_targets(pathogen_key: str, config: dict) -> list[TargetResult]:
    """Return pre-computed demo targets for a pathogen from config data."""
    eskape = config.get("eskape_pathogens", {})
    pathogen_data = eskape.get(pathogen_key)
    if not pathogen_data:
        logger.warning("No config data for pathogen: %s", pathogen_key)
        return []

    pathogen_name = pathogen_data.get("name", pathogen_key)
    resistance_desc = pathogen_data.get("resistance", "Unknown")
    targets: list[TargetResult] = []

    # Pre-computed druggability scores keyed by target name
    demo_scores: dict[str, float] = {
        "NDM-1": 0.87, "OXA-23": 0.82, "PBP3": 0.78, "LpxC": 0.91,
        "PBP2a": 0.75, "DNA Gyrase": 0.88, "KPC-2": 0.84,
        "MexB": 0.71, "LasR": 0.80,
    }

    for target_info in pathogen_data.get("key_targets", []):
        name = target_info.get("name", "Unknown")
        targets.append(
            TargetResult(
                name=name,
                pdb_id=target_info.get("pdb", ""),
                uniprot_id=target_info.get("uniprot", ""),
                function=target_info.get("function", ""),
                druggability_score=demo_scores.get(name, 0.70),
                resistance_mechanism=resistance_desc,
                confidence=0.90,
                pathogen=pathogen_name,
                inhibitors=target_info.get("inhibitors", ""),
                embedding_available=False,
            )
        )

    targets.sort(key=lambda t: t.druggability_score, reverse=True)
    return targets


class TargetAnalyzer:
    """Orchestrates target discovery using Evo2 and ESM-2 models."""

    def __init__(self, demo_mode: bool = False) -> None:
        self._config = _load_config()
        self._demo_mode = demo_mode
        self._esm2: ESM2Client | None = None

        if not demo_mode:
            self._esm2 = ESM2Client()

    def discover_targets(self, pathogen_name: str) -> list[TargetResult]:
        """Run the full target discovery pipeline for a pathogen.

        Workflow:
          1. Load pathogen data from config
          2. For each known target protein, fetch FASTA from UniProt
          3. Run ESM-2 embeddings to score druggability
          4. Optionally run Evo2 for genomic context
          5. Return ranked list of druggable targets

        Args:
            pathogen_name: Config key (e.g. 'acinetobacter_baumannii').

        Returns:
            List of TargetResult sorted by druggability_score descending.
        """
        config = self._config
        eskape = config.get("eskape_pathogens", {})
        pathogen_data = eskape.get(pathogen_name)

        if not pathogen_data:
            logger.error("Pathogen '%s' not found in config", pathogen_name)
            return []

        if self._demo_mode:
            logger.info("Demo mode: returning pre-computed targets for %s", pathogen_name)
            return _get_demo_targets(pathogen_name, config)

        pathogen_label = pathogen_data.get("name", pathogen_name)
        resistance_desc = pathogen_data.get("resistance", "Unknown")
        results: list[TargetResult] = []

        for target_info in pathogen_data.get("key_targets", []):
            name = target_info.get("name", "Unknown")
            uniprot_id = target_info.get("uniprot", "")
            pdb_id = target_info.get("pdb", "")

            # Step 2: Fetch protein sequence
            sequence = None
            if uniprot_id:
                sequence = _fetch_fasta(uniprot_id)
            if not sequence:
                sequence = _DEMO_SEQUENCES.get(uniprot_id, "MAAAVVVLLL" * 5)

            # Step 3: ESM-2 druggability scoring
            druggability = 0.70
            embedding_ok = False
            if self._esm2 and sequence:
                try:
                    druggability = self._esm2.predict_druggability(sequence)
                    embedding_ok = True
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.warning("ESM-2 scoring failed for %s: %s", name, exc)

            results.append(
                TargetResult(
                    name=name,
                    pdb_id=pdb_id,
                    uniprot_id=uniprot_id,
                    function=target_info.get("function", ""),
                    druggability_score=druggability,
                    resistance_mechanism=resistance_desc,
                    confidence=0.85 if embedding_ok else 0.60,
                    pathogen=pathogen_label,
                    inhibitors=target_info.get("inhibitors", ""),
                    embedding_available=embedding_ok,
                )
            )

        # Step 5: Sort by druggability
        results.sort(key=lambda t: t.druggability_score, reverse=True)
        logger.info(
            "Discovered %d targets for %s (top: %s, score=%.2f)",
            len(results),
            pathogen_name,
            results[0].name if results else "none",
            results[0].druggability_score if results else 0,
        )
        return results

    def discover_all_pathogens(self) -> dict[str, list[TargetResult]]:
        """Run target discovery across all ESKAPE pathogens in config.

        Returns:
            Dict mapping pathogen key to list of TargetResult.
        """
        eskape = self._config.get("eskape_pathogens", {})
        all_results: dict[str, list[TargetResult]] = {}
        for pathogen_key in eskape:
            all_results[pathogen_key] = self.discover_targets(pathogen_key)
        return all_results

--- START OF FILE: .\src\sdk\batch.py ---
"""Batch pipeline runner -- run drug discovery across multiple pathogens.

Executes the full AMR-AI pipeline for each pathogen concurrently using
ThreadPoolExecutor, then cross-compares to find broad-spectrum candidates.

Usage::

    from src.sdk.batch import BatchRunner
    runner = BatchRunner(max_workers=4, max_drugs=5)
    results = runner.run_all_pathogens(demo_mode=True)
    print(results.summary())
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import time
from collections import defaultdict
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Callable, Optional

from src.models.schemas import DrugCandidate, PipelineResults
from src.pipeline.orchestrator import PipelineOrchestrator

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
ProgressCallback = Optional[Callable[[str, str, float], None]]

ALL_PATHOGENS: list[str] = [
    "acinetobacter_baumannii",
    "staphylococcus_aureus",
    "klebsiella_pneumoniae",
    "pseudomonas_aeruginosa",
]


@dataclass
class ComparisonReport:
    """Cross-pathogen comparison of drug candidates."""

    all_candidates: dict[str, list[DrugCandidate]] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def broad_spectrum_candidates(self, min_pathogens: int = 2) -> list[dict]:
        """Drugs that scored well against multiple pathogens."""
        drug_hits: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
        for pathogen, candidates in self.all_candidates.items():
            for c in candidates:
                drug_hits[c.drug_name].append({
                    "pathogen": pathogen, "overall_score": c.overall_score,
                    "binding_affinity": c.binding_affinity,
                    "safety_score": c.safety_score, "target": c.target_name,
                })
        broad = []
        for drug_name, hits in drug_hits.items():
            if len(hits) >= min_pathogens:
                avg = sum(h["overall_score"] for h in hits) / len(hits)
                broad.append({
                    "drug_name": drug_name, "num_pathogens": len(hits),
                    "avg_overall_score": round(avg, 4),
                    "details": sorted(hits, key=lambda h: h["overall_score"], reverse=True),
                })
        broad.sort(key=lambda d: (d["num_pathogens"], d["avg_overall_score"]), reverse=True)
        return broad

    def best_per_pathogen(self) -> dict[str, dict]:
        """Top candidate per pathogen."""
        best: dict[str, dict] = {}
        for pathogen, candidates in self.all_candidates.items():
            if candidates:
                top = candidates[0]
                best[pathogen] = {
                    "drug_name": top.drug_name, "overall_score": top.overall_score,
                    "binding_affinity": top.binding_affinity,
                    "safety_score": top.safety_score, "target": top.target_name,
                }
        return best

    def format_table(self) -> str:
        """Markdown comparison table."""
        lines = ["## Cross-Pathogen Comparison", ""]
        lines.append("### Best Candidate per Pathogen")
        lines.append("| Pathogen | Drug | Target | Score |")
        lines.append("|----------|------|--------|-------|")
        for pathogen, info in self.best_per_pathogen().items():
            name = pathogen.replace("_", " ").title()
            lines.append(
                f"| {name} | {info['drug_name']} | {info['target']} "
                f"| {info['overall_score']:.3f} |")
        lines.append("")
        broad = self.broad_spectrum_candidates()
        if broad:
            lines.append("### Broad-Spectrum Candidates (active against 2+ pathogens)")
            lines.append("| Drug | Pathogens | Avg Score |")
            lines.append("|------|-----------|-----------|")
            for entry in broad[:10]:
                plist = ", ".join(d["pathogen"].split("_")[0].title() for d in entry["details"])
                lines.append(
                    f"| {entry['drug_name']} | {entry['num_pathogens']} ({plist}) "
                    f"| {entry['avg_overall_score']:.3f} |")
            lines.append("")
        return "\n".join(lines)


@dataclass
class BatchResults:
    """Aggregated results from batch pipeline runs."""

    pathogen_results: dict[str, PipelineResults] = field(default_factory=dict)
    errors: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    total_duration: float = 0.0

    @property
    def total_targets(self) -> int:
        return sum(len(r.targets) for r in self.pathogen_results.values())

    @property
    def total_candidates(self) -> int:
        return sum(len(r.top_candidates) for r in self.pathogen_results.values())

    @property
    def cross_reactive_drugs(self) -> list[dict]:
        """Drugs effective against 2+ pathogens."""
        return self.compare_results().broad_spectrum_candidates()

    def compare_results(self) -> ComparisonReport:
        """Build a ComparisonReport from all pathogen results."""
        return ComparisonReport(all_candidates={
            p: r.top_candidates for p, r in self.pathogen_results.items()
        })

    def summary(self) -> str:
        """Markdown summary of the batch run."""
        lines = [
            "# Batch Pipeline Results", "",
            f"- **Pathogens analysed:** {len(self.pathogen_results)}",
            f"- **Total targets:** {self.total_targets}",
            f"- **Total candidates:** {self.total_candidates}",
            f"- **Broad-spectrum drugs:** {len(self.cross_reactive_drugs)}",
            f"- **Duration:** {self.total_duration:.1f}s", "",
        ]
        if self.errors:
            lines.append("## Errors")
            for pathogen, err in self.errors.items():
                lines.append(f"- **{pathogen}:** {err}")
            lines.append("")
        for pathogen, result in self.pathogen_results.items():
            name = pathogen.replace("_", " ").title()
            lines.append(f"## {name}")
            lines.append(f"- Targets: {len(result.targets)}, Candidates: {len(result.top_candidates)}")
            for c in result.top_candidates[:3]:
                lines.append(f"  - {c.drug_name} (score={c.overall_score:.3f}, bind={c.binding_affinity:.3f})")
            lines.append("")
        lines.append(self.compare_results().format_table())
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def to_json(self, indent: int = 2) -> str:
        """JSON export of all results."""
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "total_duration": self.total_duration,
            "total_targets": self.total_targets,
            "total_candidates": self.total_candidates,
            "cross_reactive_drugs": self.cross_reactive_drugs,
            "errors": self.errors,
            "pathogens": {p: r.to_dict() for p, r in self.pathogen_results.items()},
        }
        return json.dumps(payload, indent=indent, default=str)


class BatchRunner:
    """Run pipelines across multiple targets/pathogens in parallel."""

    def __init__(self, max_workers: int = 4, max_drugs: int = 5) -> None:
        self._max_workers = max_workers
        self._max_drugs = max_drugs

    def run_all_pathogens(
        self,
        pathogens: list[str] | None = None,
        progress_callback: ProgressCallback = None,
        demo_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> BatchResults:
        """Run the full pipeline for each pathogen in parallel.

        Args:
            pathogens:         Pathogen identifiers, or None for ALL_PATHOGENS.
            progress_callback: ``(pathogen, stage, pct)`` reporter.
            demo_mode:         If True, skip API calls (pure heuristic).
        """
        targets = pathogens or ALL_PATHOGENS
        batch = BatchResults()
        start = time.time()

        def _run_one(pathogen: str) -> tuple[str, PipelineResults]:
            cb = None
            if progress_callback:
                def cb(stage: str, pct: float, msg: str) -> None:
                    progress_callback(pathogen, stage, pct)
            orch = PipelineOrchestrator(progress_callback=cb)
            return pathogen, orch.run_full_pipeline(
                pathogen_name=pathogen, max_drugs=self._max_drugs, demo_mode=demo_mode,
            )

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=self._max_workers) as pool:
            futures = {pool.submit(_run_one, p): p for p in targets}
            for future in as_completed(futures):
                pathogen = futures[future]
                try:
                    _, result = future.result()
                    batch.pathogen_results[pathogen] = result
                    logger.info("Completed %s: %d candidates", pathogen, len(result.top_candidates))
                except Exception as exc:
                    batch.errors[pathogen] = str(exc)
                    logger.error("Failed %s: %s", pathogen, exc)

        batch.total_duration = round(time.time() - start, 2)
        return batch

    def run_multi_target(
        self,
        targets: list[dict[str, str]],
        drug_library: list[dict[str, str]],
        demo_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> BatchResults:
        """Screen a drug library against multiple explicit targets.

        Each entry in *targets* should have a ``pathogen`` key.
        """
        pathogens = [t["pathogen"] for t in targets if "pathogen" in t]
        return self.run_all_pathogens(pathogens=pathogens, demo_mode=demo_mode)
