--- START OF FILE: .\src\sdk\optimizer.py ---
"""Closed-loop molecule optimization engine.

Runs Generate -> Dock -> Score -> Mutate -> Repeat using NVIDIA NIM
(MolMIM for generation, Boltz-2 for binding) and RDKit for drug-likeness.

Usage::

    from resistai.optimizer import MoleculeOptimizer

    optimizer = MoleculeOptimizer(
        target_sequence="MELPNIMH...",
        seed_smiles="OB(O)C1CCC(CC(=O)NC2CCNC2)CC1",
        objectives=["binding", "qed", "lipinski", "toxicity"],
        max_iterations=5,
        molecules_per_iteration=5,
    )
    results = optimizer.run()
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Sequence

from src.models.nim_client import NIMClient
from src.utils.chemistry import (
    compute_descriptors,
    passes_lipinski,
    tanimoto_similarity,
    validate_smiles,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

VALID_OBJECTIVES = frozenset({"binding", "qed", "lipinski", "toxicity"})


@dataclass
class Candidate:
    """A molecule candidate with multi-objective scores and lineage."""

    smiles: str
    scores: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    parent_smiles: Optional[str] = None
    generation: int = 0
    raw_molmim: Optional[dict] = None

    def dominates(self, other: "Candidate", objectives: Sequence[str]) -> bool:
        """True if self >= other on all objectives and strictly > on at least one."""
        better_count = 0
        for obj in objectives:
            s, o = self.scores.get(obj, 0.0), other.scores.get(obj, 0.0)
            if s < o:
                return False
            if s > o:
                better_count += 1
        return better_count > 0

    def weighted_score(self, weights: Dict[str, float]) -> float:
        return sum(self.scores.get(k, 0.0) * w for k, w in weights.items())

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        sc = ", ".join(f"{k}={v:.3f}" for k, v in self.scores.items())
        return f"Candidate({self.smiles!r}, gen={self.generation}, {sc})"


@dataclass
class OptimizationResult:
    """Aggregate output of the optimisation loop."""

    all_candidates: List[Candidate] = field(default_factory=list)
    pareto_frontier: List[Candidate] = field(default_factory=list)
    best_per_objective: Dict[str, Candidate] = field(default_factory=dict)
    history: List[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    iterations_run: int = 0

    def summary(self) -> str:
        lines = [
            f"Optimization complete: {self.iterations_run} iterations, "
            f"{len(self.all_candidates)} total candidates",
            f"Pareto frontier size: {len(self.pareto_frontier)}",
        ]
        for obj, cand in self.best_per_objective.items():
            lines.append(f"  Best {obj}: {cand.scores.get(obj, 0):.4f}  ({cand.smiles})")
        return "\n".join(lines)


# -- Scoring helpers ----------------------------------------------------------

def _score_toxicity(smiles: str) -> float:
    """Heuristic toxicity score [0,1] -- higher means less toxic (better)."""
    desc = compute_descriptors(smiles)
    if desc is None:
        return 0.0
    penalty = 0.0
    mw = desc["MW"]
    if mw > 500:
        penalty += min((mw - 500) / 500, 0.4)
    elif mw < 150:
        penalty += 0.2
    logp = desc["LogP"]
    if logp > 5:
        penalty += min((logp - 5) / 5, 0.3)
    elif logp < -1:
        penalty += 0.1
    if desc["TPSA"] > 140:
        penalty += 0.15
    if desc["RotBonds"] > 10:
        penalty += 0.1
    return max(0.0, 1.0 - penalty)


def _extract_qed(mol_dict: dict) -> float:
    """Extract QED from a MolMIM molecule dict."""
    for key in ("qed", "score"):
        if key in mol_dict:
            return float(mol_dict[key])
    return 0.0


def compute_pareto_frontier(
    candidates: List[Candidate], objectives: Sequence[str],
) -> List[Candidate]:
    """Return candidates not dominated by any other candidate."""
    frontier: List[Candidate] = []
    for cand in candidates:
        if not any(
            other.dominates(cand, objectives)
            for other in candidates
            if other is not cand
        ):
            frontier.append(cand)
    return frontier


# -- Main optimiser -----------------------------------------------------------

class MoleculeOptimizer:
    """Closed-loop multi-objective molecule optimiser.

    Args:
        target_sequence: Amino-acid sequence of the protein target.
        seed_smiles: Starting SMILES (e.g. Taniborbactam).
        objectives: Objectives to maximise (binding, qed, lipinski, toxicity).
        max_iterations: Generate-score-select rounds.
        molecules_per_iteration: Variants per seed per round.
        weights: Objective -> weight mapping for scalar ranking.
        min_similarity: Min Tanimoto similarity for MolMIM generation.
        top_k: Seeds carried forward each iteration.
        nim_client: Pre-configured NIMClient (auto-created if omitted).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        target_sequence: str,
        seed_smiles: str,
        objectives: Optional[List[str]] = None,
        max_iterations: int = 5,
        molecules_per_iteration: int = 5,
        weights: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None,
        min_similarity: float = 0.3,
        top_k: int = 3,
        nim_client: Optional[NIMClient] = None,
    ) -> None:
        if not target_sequence:
            raise ValueError("target_sequence must be a non-empty protein sequence")
        if not validate_smiles(seed_smiles):
            raise ValueError(f"seed_smiles is not a valid SMILES: {seed_smiles!r}")

        self.target_sequence = target_sequence
        self.seed_smiles = seed_smiles
        self.objectives = list(objectives or ["binding", "qed", "lipinski", "toxicity"])
        for obj in self.objectives:
            if obj not in VALID_OBJECTIVES:
                raise ValueError(f"Unknown objective {obj!r}; valid: {sorted(VALID_OBJECTIVES)}")

        self.max_iterations = max_iterations
        self.molecules_per_iteration = molecules_per_iteration
        self.min_similarity = min_similarity
        self.top_k = top_k
        self.nim = nim_client or NIMClient()

        if weights is not None:
            self.weights = weights
        else:
            w = 1.0 / len(self.objectives)
            self.weights = {obj: w for obj in self.objectives}

    def _generate_variants(self, seed: str) -> List[dict]:
        """Call MolMIM to generate variants from a seed SMILES."""
        result = self.nim.generate_molecules(
            seed_smiles=seed,
            num_molecules=self.molecules_per_iteration,
            property_name="QED",
            min_similarity=self.min_similarity,
        )
        if not result.success:
            logger.warning("MolMIM generation failed for seed %s", seed)
            return []
        return result.molecules

    def _score_binding(self, smiles: str) -> float:
        """Score binding affinity via Boltz-2 confidence (0-1)."""
        result = self.nim.predict_binding(
            protein_sequence=self.target_sequence,
            ligand_smiles=smiles,
        )
        if not result.success:
            logger.debug("Boltz-2 failed for %s", smiles)
            return 0.0
        return result.confidence_score

    def _score_candidate(self, smiles: str, mol_dict: dict) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Compute all requested objective scores for a molecule."""
        scores: Dict[str, float] = {}
        if "binding" in self.objectives:
            scores["binding"] = self._score_binding(smiles)
        if "qed" in self.objectives:
            scores["qed"] = _extract_qed(mol_dict)
        if "lipinski" in self.objectives:
            scores["lipinski"] = 1.0 if passes_lipinski(smiles) else 0.0
        if "toxicity" in self.objectives:
            scores["toxicity"] = _score_toxicity(smiles)
        return scores

    def _select_seeds(self, candidates: List[Candidate]) -> List[str]:
        """Pick top-k unique candidates by weighted score as next seeds."""
        ranked = sorted(candidates, key=lambda c: c.weighted_score(self.weights), reverse=True)
        seen: set[str] = set()
        seeds: List[str] = []
        for cand in ranked:
            if cand.smiles not in seen:
                seen.add(cand.smiles)
                seeds.append(cand.smiles)
            if len(seeds) >= self.top_k:
                break
        return seeds

    def _best_weighted(self, candidates: List[Candidate]) -> float:
        if not candidates:
            return -math.inf
        return max(c.weighted_score(self.weights) for c in candidates)

    def run(self) -> OptimizationResult:
        """Execute the full closed-loop optimisation.

        Returns an OptimizationResult with all candidates, the Pareto
        frontier, best-per-objective, and per-iteration history.
        """
        all_candidates: List[Candidate] = []
        history: List[dict] = []
        seeds = [self.seed_smiles]
        no_improve_count = 0
        prev_best = -math.inf

        for iteration in range(1, self.max_iterations + 1):
            logger.info("=== Iteration %d/%d | seeds: %d ===", iteration, self.max_iterations, len(seeds))
            iter_candidates: List[Candidate] = []
            all_smiles = {c.smiles for c in all_candidates}

            for seed in seeds:
                for mol_dict in self._generate_variants(seed):
                    smiles = mol_dict.get("smiles", mol_dict.get("smi", ""))
                    if not smiles or not validate_smiles(smiles):
                        continue
                    if smiles in all_smiles or any(c.smiles == smiles for c in iter_candidates):
                        continue

                    scores = self._score_candidate(smiles, mol_dict)
                    iter_candidates.append(Candidate(
                        smiles=smiles, scores=scores,
                        parent_smiles=seed, generation=iteration,
                        raw_molmim=mol_dict,
                    ))

            all_candidates.extend(iter_candidates)
            iter_best = self._best_weighted(iter_candidates) if iter_candidates else 0.0
            history.append({
                "iteration": iteration,
                "seeds": list(seeds),
                "num_generated": len(iter_candidates),
                "best_weighted_score": iter_best,
                "candidates": [{"smiles": c.smiles, "scores": dict(c.scores)} for c in iter_candidates],
            })
            logger.info("  Generated %d candidates | best weighted=%.4f", len(iter_candidates), iter_best)

            # Early stopping: no improvement for 2 consecutive iterations
            if iter_best <= prev_best:
                no_improve_count += 1
                if no_improve_count >= 2:
                    logger.info("Early stopping: no improvement for %d iterations", no_improve_count)
                    break
            else:
                no_improve_count = 0
                prev_best = iter_best

            seeds = self._select_seeds(all_candidates)
            if not seeds:
                logger.warning("No viable seeds; stopping early")
                break

        # Build final result
        pareto = compute_pareto_frontier(all_candidates, self.objectives)
        best_per_obj: Dict[str, Candidate] = {}
        for obj in self.objectives:
            if all_candidates:
                best_per_obj[obj] = max(all_candidates, key=lambda c, o=obj: c.scores.get(o, 0.0))

        result = OptimizationResult(
            all_candidates=all_candidates, pareto_frontier=pareto,
            best_per_objective=best_per_obj, history=history,
            iterations_run=len(history),
        )
        logger.info(result.summary())
        return result

--- END OF FILE: .\src\sdk\optimizer.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\sdk\pipeline.py ---
"""ResistAI Pipeline SDK -- composable, chainable drug-discovery pipeline.

Wraps the existing NIMClient and NebisLLM into a lazy-execution pipeline
that queues steps and runs them in order when ``.run()`` is called.

Usage::

    from src.sdk import Pipeline, Target

    target = Target.from_sequence("MELPNIMH...", name="NDM-1")
    results = (
        Pipeline(target)
        .fold()
        .screen(drugs=[{"name": "Meropenem", "smiles": "CC(...)O"}])
        .dock(top_k=3)
        .generate(n=5, optimize="QED")
        .validate()
        .design_binder()
        .analyze()
        .run(progress=True)
    )
    print(results.to_report())
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import sys
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Callable, Optional, Sequence

from src.sdk.results import (
    BinderDesign,
    DockingPose,
    NovelMolecule,
    PipelineResults,
    ScreeningHit,
    ValidationResult,
)
from src.sdk.target import Target

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Step descriptors (internal)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class _Step:
    """A single queued pipeline step."""

    name: str
    fn: Callable[[PipelineResults], None]
    kwargs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Default drug library (fallback when no drugs list supplied)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_DEFAULT_DRUGS: list[dict[str, str]] = [
    {"name": "Meropenem", "smiles": "CC(C1C2CC(=C(N2C1=O)C(=O)O)SC3CC(NC3)C(=O)N(C)C)O"},
    {"name": "Ciprofloxacin", "smiles": "C1CC1N2C=C(C(=O)C3=CC(=C(C=C32)N4CCNCC4)F)C(=O)O"},
    {"name": "Amoxicillin", "smiles": "CC1(C(N2C(S1)C(C2=O)NC(=O)C(C3=CC=C(C=C3)O)N)C(=O)O)C"},
    {"name": "Linezolid", "smiles": "CC(=O)NCC1CN(C(=O)O1)C2=CC(=C(C=C2)N3CCOCC3)F"},
    {"name": "Doxycycline", "smiles": "CC1C2C(C3C(C(=O)C(=C(C3(C(=O)C2=C(C4=C1C=CC=C4O)O)O)O)C(=O)N)N(C)C)O"},
]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Pipeline
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class Pipeline:
    """Lazy, chainable drug-discovery pipeline backed by NVIDIA NIM.

    Steps are recorded when their methods are called but only executed
    when ``.run()`` is invoked, in the order they were added.

    Args:
        target: The protein target to run the pipeline against.
    """

    def __init__(self, target: Target) -> None:
        self._target = target
        self._steps: list[_Step] = []
        self._nim: Any = None  # lazy NIMClient
        self._llm: Any = None  # lazy NebisLLM

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Lazy client accessors
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _get_nim(self) -> Any:
        """Return (or create) the NIMClient singleton for this pipeline."""
        if self._nim is None:
            from src.models.nim_client import NIMClient
            self._nim = NIMClient()
        return self._nim

    def _get_llm(self) -> Any:
        """Return (or create) the NebisLLM singleton for this pipeline."""
        if self._llm is None:
            from src.models.llm_client import NebisLLM
            self._llm = NebisLLM()
        return self._llm

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Step builders (each returns self for chaining)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def fold(self) -> Pipeline:
        """Queue ESMFold structure prediction for the target sequence."""

        def _run_fold(results: PipelineResults) -> None:
            from src.pipeline.stages import stage_fold_protein

            nim = self._get_nim()
            fold = stage_fold_protein(nim, self._target.sequence)
            if fold["success"]:
                results.structure = fold["pdb_string"]
                results.structure_length = len(fold["pdb_string"])
                results.fold_elapsed_ms = fold["elapsed_ms"]
                self._target.pdb_string = fold["pdb_string"]
            elif not nim or not nim.available:
                results.errors.append({"step": "fold", "error": "NIM client unavailable (no API key)"})
            else:
                results.errors.append({"step": "fold", "error": "ESMFold returned no structure"})

        self._steps.append(_Step(name="fold", fn=_run_fold))
        return self

    def screen(
        self,
        drugs: Optional[list[dict[str, str]]] = None,
    ) -> Pipeline:
        """Queue Boltz-2 binding screening against a drug library.

        Args:
            drugs: List of dicts with ``name`` and ``smiles`` keys.
                   Falls back to a built-in antibiotic library when omitted.
        """
        drug_list = drugs if drugs is not None else _DEFAULT_DRUGS

        def _run_screen(results: PipelineResults) -> None:
            from src.pipeline.stages import stage_screen_drug

            nim = self._get_nim()
            if not nim or not nim.available:
                results.errors.append({"step": "screen", "error": "NIM client unavailable"})
                return
            for drug in drug_list:
                name = drug.get("name", "unknown")
                smiles = drug.get("smiles", "")
                if not smiles:
                    continue
                binding = stage_screen_drug(nim, self._target.sequence, smiles)
                if binding["success"]:
                    results.screening_hits.append(ScreeningHit(
                        drug_name=name,
                        smiles=smiles,
                        confidence=binding["confidence"],
                        ptm_score=binding["ptm_score"],
                        iptm_score=binding["iptm_score"],
                        ligand_iptm=binding["ligand_iptm"],
                        structure_cif=binding["structure_cif"],
                        raw=binding["raw"],
                    ))
                else:
                    results.errors.append({"step": "screen", "drug": name, "error": "Boltz-2 binding failed"})
            # Sort by confidence descending
            results.screening_hits.sort(key=lambda h: h.confidence, reverse=True)

        self._steps.append(_Step(name="screen", fn=_run_screen))
        return self

    def dock(self, top_k: int = 5, num_poses: int = 5) -> Pipeline:
        """Queue DiffDock 3D docking for the top screening hits.

        Requires a PDB structure (from ``fold()`` or the target itself).

        Args:
            top_k:     Number of top screening hits to dock.
            num_poses: Number of poses per ligand.
        """

        def _run_dock(results: PipelineResults) -> None:
            from src.pipeline.stages import stage_dock as _stage_dock

            nim = self._get_nim()
            if not nim or not nim.available:
                results.errors.append({"step": "dock", "error": "NIM client unavailable"})
                return
            pdb = self._target.pdb_string or (results.structure or "")
            if not pdb or len(pdb) < 100:
                results.errors.append({
                    "step": "dock",
                    "error": "No PDB structure available; run fold() first or use Target.from_pdb()",
                })
                return
            # Pick candidates from screening hits, or use default drugs
            candidates: list[dict[str, str]] = []
            if results.screening_hits:
                for hit in results.screening_hits[:top_k]:
                    candidates.append({"name": hit.drug_name, "smiles": hit.smiles})
            else:
                candidates = _DEFAULT_DRUGS[:top_k]

            for drug in candidates:
                name = drug.get("name", "unknown")
                smiles = drug.get("smiles", "")
                if not smiles:
                    continue
                dock = _stage_dock(nim, pdb, smiles, num_poses=num_poses)
                if dock["success"]:
                    for i, pos in enumerate(dock["ligand_positions"]):
                        conf = (
                            dock["position_confidence"][i]
                            if i < len(dock["position_confidence"])
                            else 0.0
                        )
                        results.docking_poses.append(DockingPose(
                            ligand_sdf=pos,
                            confidence=conf,
                            drug_name=name,
                            smiles=smiles,
                        ))
                else:
                    results.errors.append({"step": "dock", "drug": name, "error": "DiffDock docking failed"})
            # Sort by confidence descending
            results.docking_poses.sort(key=lambda p: p.confidence, reverse=True)

        self._steps.append(_Step(name="dock", fn=_run_dock))
        return self

    def generate(
        self,
        n: int = 10,
        optimize: str = "QED",
        seed_smiles: Optional[str] = None,
        min_similarity: float = 0.3,
    ) -> Pipeline:
        """Queue MolMIM novel molecule generation.

        Args:
            n:              Number of molecules to generate.
            optimize:       Property to optimize (``"QED"``, ``"logP"``, etc.).
            seed_smiles:    Starting SMILES. Defaults to top screening hit.
            min_similarity: Minimum Tanimoto similarity to seed.
        """

        def _run_generate(results: PipelineResults) -> None:
            from src.pipeline.stages import stage_generate_molecules

            nim = self._get_nim()
            if not nim or not nim.available:
                results.errors.append({"step": "generate", "error": "NIM client unavailable"})
                return
            seed = seed_smiles
            if not seed:
                if results.screening_hits:
                    seed = results.screening_hits[0].smiles
                else:
                    seed = _DEFAULT_DRUGS[0]["smiles"]
            gen = stage_generate_molecules(
                nim, seed_smiles=seed, num_molecules=n,
                property_name=optimize, min_similarity=min_similarity,
            )
            if gen["success"]:
                for mol in gen["molecules"]:
                    results.novel_molecules.append(NovelMolecule(
                        smiles=mol["smiles"],
                        score=float(mol["score"]),
                        score_type=gen["score_type"],
                        seed_smiles=seed,
                    ))
            else:
                results.errors.append({"step": "generate", "error": "MolMIM returned no molecules"})

        self._steps.append(_Step(name="generate", fn=_run_generate))
        return self

    def validate(self) -> Pipeline:
        """Queue Boltz-2 binding validation for generated novel molecules."""

        def _run_validate(results: PipelineResults) -> None:
            from src.pipeline.stages import stage_screen_drug

            nim = self._get_nim()
            if not nim or not nim.available:
                results.errors.append({"step": "validate", "error": "NIM client unavailable"})
                return
            mols = results.novel_molecules
            if not mols:
                results.errors.append({"step": "validate", "error": "No novel molecules to validate"})
                return
            for mol in mols:
                if not mol.smiles:
                    continue
                binding = stage_screen_drug(nim, self._target.sequence, mol.smiles)
                if binding["success"]:
                    results.validated_molecules.append(ValidationResult(
                        smiles=mol.smiles,
                        confidence=binding["confidence"],
                        iptm_score=binding["iptm_score"],
                        success=True,
                    ))
                else:
                    results.errors.append({"step": "validate", "smiles": mol.smiles[:40], "error": "Boltz-2 validation failed"})
            results.validated_molecules.sort(key=lambda v: v.confidence, reverse=True)

        self._steps.append(_Step(name="validate", fn=_run_validate))
        return self

    def design_binder(self, contigs: str = "10-40/A1-60/10-40") -> Pipeline:
        """Queue RFdiffusion backbone design + ProteinMPNN sequence design.

        Requires a PDB structure on the target.

        Args:
            contigs: RFdiffusion contig specification.
        """

        def _run_design(results: PipelineResults) -> None:
            from src.pipeline.stages import stage_design_binder

            nim = self._get_nim()
            if not nim or not nim.available:
                results.errors.append({"step": "design_binder", "error": "NIM client unavailable"})
                return
            pdb = self._target.pdb_string or (results.structure or "")
            if not pdb or len(pdb) < 100:
                results.errors.append({
                    "step": "design_binder",
                    "error": "No PDB structure; run fold() first",
                })
                return
            binder = stage_design_binder(nim, pdb, contigs=contigs)
            if binder["success"]:
                results.binder_design = BinderDesign(
                    backbone_pdb=binder["backbone_pdb"],
                    sequences=binder["sequences"],
                    success=True,
                )
            else:
                results.errors.append({"step": "design_binder", "error": "RFdiffusion/ProteinMPNN failed"})
                results.binder_design = BinderDesign(success=False)

        self._steps.append(_Step(name="design_binder", fn=_run_design))
        return self

    def analyze(self, prompt: Optional[str] = None) -> Pipeline:
        """Queue LLM-powered analysis of all pipeline results so far.

        Args:
            prompt: Custom prompt override. By default a comprehensive
                    summary prompt is generated from the results.
        """

        def _run_analyze(results: PipelineResults) -> None:
            llm = self._get_llm()
            if not llm.available:
                results.errors.append({"step": "analyze", "error": "LLM client unavailable"})
                return
            if prompt:
                text = llm.complete(prompt)
            else:
                text = self._auto_analyze(results, llm)
            results.analysis = text or None

        self._steps.append(_Step(name="analyze", fn=_run_analyze))
        return self

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Execution
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def run(self, progress: bool = False) -> PipelineResults:
        """Execute all queued steps in order and return results.

        Args:
            progress: If ``True``, print step names and timing to stderr.
        """
        results = PipelineResults(
            target_name=self._target.name,
            target_sequence=self._target.sequence,
            started_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        )

        for step in self._steps:
            if progress:
                print(f"  [{step.name}] running...", end="", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
            t0 = time.time()
            try:
                step.fn(results)
            except Exception as exc:
                results.errors.append({"step": step.name, "error": str(exc)})
                logger.error("Step %s failed: %s", step.name, exc)
            elapsed = time.time() - t0
            results.steps_executed.append(step.name)
            if progress:
                print(f" done ({elapsed:.1f}s)", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)

        results.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        return results

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Internals
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _auto_analyze(self, results: PipelineResults, llm: Any) -> str:
        """Build and run a comprehensive analysis prompt from results."""
        parts: list[str] = [
            f"Analyze these drug-discovery pipeline results for target '{self._target.name}'",
            f"(sequence length: {self._target.length} aa, source: {self._target.source}).",
            "",
        ]
        if results.structure is not None:
            parts.append(f"- Structure predicted via ESMFold ({results.structure_length} chars)")
        if results.screening_hits:
            top = results.screening_hits[:5]
            hits_str = ", ".join(
                f"{h.drug_name} (conf={h.confidence:.3f})" for h in top
            )
            parts.append(f"- Screening: {len(results.screening_hits)} drugs tested, top hits: {hits_str}")
        if results.docking_poses:
            parts.append(f"- Docking: {len(results.docking_poses)} poses generated")
        if results.novel_molecules:
            parts.append(f"- MolMIM generated {len(results.novel_molecules)} novel molecules")
        if results.validated_molecules:
            top_v = results.validated_molecules[:3]
            v_str = ", ".join(f"conf={v.confidence:.3f}" for v in top_v)
            parts.append(f"- Validation: {len(results.validated_molecules)} molecules validated, top: {v_str}")
        if results.binder_design:
            parts.append(f"- Binder design success: {results.binder_design.success}")
        parts.append("")
        parts.append(
            "Provide: (1) key findings, (2) most promising candidates and why, "
            "(3) recommended next experimental steps, (4) limitations of this computational analysis."
        )
        full_prompt = "\n".join(parts)
        system = (
            "You are an expert computational biologist interpreting an AI-driven "
            "antimicrobial drug discovery pipeline. Be concise and actionable."
        )
        return llm.complete(full_prompt, system=system, max_tokens=1500)

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        step_names = [s.name for s in self._steps]
        return f"Pipeline(target={self._target.name!r}, steps={step_names})"

--- END OF FILE: .\src\sdk\pipeline.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\sdk\report_pdf.py ---
"""HTML/PDF report generator for ResistAI pipeline results."""
from __future__ import annotations

import html
import logging
import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_VERSION = "1.0.0"


def _esc(text: Any) -> str:
    return html.escape(str(text)) if text else ""


def _badge(label: str, color: str = "#00C4B4") -> str:
    return (f'<span style="background:{color};color:#fff;padding:2px 8px;'
            f'border-radius:4px;font-size:0.8em;margin-right:4px;">{_esc(label)}</span>')


def _pct_bar(value: float, max_val: float = 1.0) -> str:
    pct = min(100, max(0, (value / max_val) * 100)) if max_val else 0
    color = "#00C4B4" if pct >= 60 else "#F59E0B" if pct >= 30 else "#F43F5E"
    return (f'<div style="background:#2a2a3e;border-radius:4px;height:14px;width:100px;'
            f'display:inline-block;vertical-align:middle;">'
            f'<div style="background:{color};height:100%;width:{pct:.0f}%;'
            f'border-radius:4px;"></div></div> {pct:.0f}%')


class ReportBuilder:
    """Generates polished HTML/PDF reports from pipeline results."""

    def __init__(self, title: str = "ResistAI Discovery Report"):
        self._title = title
        self._sections: list[dict[str, str]] = []

    def add_executive_summary(
        self, pathogen: str, targets: int, candidates: int,
        models_used: list[str], duration: float, llm_summary: str = "",
    ) -> ReportBuilder:
        badges = "".join(_badge(m) for m in models_used)
        body = (
            f"<p><strong>Pathogen:</strong> {_esc(pathogen)}</p>"
            f"<p><strong>Targets identified:</strong> {targets} &nbsp;|&nbsp; "
            f"<strong>Drug candidates:</strong> {candidates} &nbsp;|&nbsp; "
            f"<strong>Duration:</strong> {duration:.1f}s</p>"
            f"<p><strong>Models:</strong> {badges}</p>"
        )
        if llm_summary:
            body += f"<blockquote>{_esc(llm_summary)}</blockquote>"
        self._sections.append({"heading": "Executive Summary", "body": body})
        return self

    def add_target_table(self, targets: list[dict]) -> ReportBuilder:
        if not targets:
            return self
        rows = "".join(
            f"<tr><td>{_esc(t.get('name',''))}</td><td><code>{_esc(t.get('pdb_code',''))}</code></td>"
            f"<td>{_esc(t.get('uniprot_id',''))}</td><td>{_pct_bar(t.get('druggability_score',0))}</td>"
            f"<td>{_esc(t.get('function',''))}</td><td>{_esc(t.get('resistance_mechanism',''))}</td></tr>"
            for t in targets)
        self._sections.append({"heading": "Target Discovery", "body": (
            "<table><thead><tr><th>Target</th><th>PDB</th><th>UniProt</th>"
            f"<th>Druggability</th><th>Function</th><th>Resistance</th></tr></thead>"
            f"<tbody>{rows}</tbody></table>")})
        return self

    def add_candidate_table(self, candidates: list[dict]) -> ReportBuilder:
        if not candidates:
            return self
        rows = ""
        for c in candidates:
            rows += (
                f"<tr><td>{_esc(c.get('rank', ''))}</td>"
                f"<td><strong>{_esc(c.get('drug_name', ''))}</strong></td>"
                f"<td><code>{_esc(c.get('smiles', '')[:50])}</code></td>"
                f"<td>{_esc(c.get('target_name', ''))}</td>"
                f"<td>{c.get('binding_affinity', 0):.3f}</td>"
                f"<td>{_pct_bar(c.get('safety_score', 0))}</td>"
                f"<td><strong>{c.get('overall_score', 0):.3f}</strong></td>"
                f"<td>{_esc(c.get('regulatory_pathway', ''))}</td></tr>"
            )
        body = (
            "<table><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>Drug</th><th>SMILES</th>"
            "<th>Target</th><th>Affinity</th><th>Safety</th>"
            "<th>Score</th><th>Pathway</th>"
            f"</tr></thead><tbody>{rows}</tbody></table>"
        )
        self._sections.append({"heading": "Ranked Drug Candidates", "body": body})
        return self

    def add_novel_molecules(self, molecules: list[dict]) -> ReportBuilder:
        if not molecules:
            return self
        rows = ""
        for i, m in enumerate(molecules, 1):
            smi = str(m.get("smiles", ""))
            rows += (
                f"<tr><td>{i}</td>"
                f"<td><code>{_esc(smi[:60])}</code></td>"
                f"<td>{m.get('score', 0):.3f}</td>"
                f"<td>{_esc(m.get('score_type', m.get('type', '')))}</td>"
                f"<td><code>{_esc(str(m.get('seed_smiles', ''))[:40])}</code></td></tr>"
            )
        body = (
            "<table><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>SMILES</th><th>Score</th>"
            f"<th>Type</th><th>Seed</th></tr></thead><tbody>{rows}</tbody></table>"
        )
        self._sections.append({"heading": "AI-Generated Novel Molecules", "body": body})
        return self

    def add_binder_design(self, designs: list[dict]) -> ReportBuilder:
        if not designs:
            return self
        items = ""
        for d in designs:
            status = "Success" if d.get("success") else "Failed"
            color = "#00C4B4" if d.get("success") else "#F43F5E"
            seqs = d.get("sequences", [])
            items += (
                f'<div style="border-left:3px solid {color};padding:8px 12px;'
                f'margin-bottom:8px;background:#2a2a3e;border-radius:0 4px 4px 0;">'
                f"<strong>{status}</strong> -- {len(seqs)} designed sequence(s)"
            )
            for s in seqs[:3]:
                items += f"<br><code>{_esc(str(s)[:80])}</code>"
            items += "</div>"
        self._sections.append({"heading": "De Novo Binder Design", "body": items})
        return self

    def add_safety_profile(self, safety_data: list[dict]) -> ReportBuilder:
        if not safety_data:
            return self
        rows = ""
        for s in safety_data:
            rec = s.get("recommendation", "?")
            rec_color = "#00C4B4" if rec == "proceed" else "#F59E0B" if rec == "caution" else "#F43F5E"
            flags = ", ".join(s.get("flags", [])) or "None"
            rows += (
                f"<tr><td><strong>{_esc(s.get('drug_name', ''))}</strong></td>"
                f"<td>{_pct_bar(s.get('safety_score', 0))}</td>"
                f"<td>{_esc(s.get('toxicity_score', 'N/A'))}</td>"
                f"<td>{_esc(flags)}</td>"
                f'<td style="color:{rec_color};font-weight:bold;">{_esc(rec)}</td></tr>'
            )
        body = (
            "<table><thead><tr><th>Drug</th><th>Safety</th><th>Toxicity</th>"
            f"<th>Flags</th><th>Rec.</th></tr></thead><tbody>{rows}</tbody></table>"
        )
        self._sections.append({"heading": "Safety & ADMET Profile", "body": body})
        return self

    def add_regulatory_pathway(self) -> ReportBuilder:
        self._sections.append({"heading": "Regulatory Pathway", "body": (
            '<div style="background:#2a2a3e;padding:16px;border-radius:8px;">'
            "<p><strong>Recommended:</strong> FDA 505(b)(2)</p><ul>"
            "<li>Leverages existing safety data for known scaffolds</li>"
            "<li>Reduced clinical trial requirements vs. NDA</li>"
            "<li>Estimated timeline: 3-5 years to approval</li>"
            "<li>AI-generated evidence package included</li></ul>"
            "<p><strong>WHO GLASS:</strong> Targets from WHO Priority Pathogen List.</p></div>"
        )})
        return self

    def add_cost_analysis(self, cost_data: dict) -> ReportBuilder:
        trad = cost_data.get("traditional_cost", 2_600_000_000)
        ai = cost_data.get("ai_cost", cost_data.get("total_cost", 0))
        savings = ((trad - ai) / trad * 100) if trad else 0
        card = ('<div style="background:#2a2a3e;padding:16px;border-radius:8px;'
                'flex:1;min-width:180px;text-align:center;">')
        body = (
            f'<div style="display:flex;gap:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;">'
            f'{card}<div style="font-size:0.85em;color:#999;">Traditional R&D</div>'
            f'<div style="font-size:1.6em;color:#e04040;">${trad:,.0f}</div></div>'
            f'{card}<div style="font-size:0.85em;color:#999;">AI-Accelerated</div>'
            f'<div style="font-size:1.6em;color:#00C4B4;">${ai:,.2f}</div></div>'
            f'{card}<div style="font-size:0.85em;color:#999;">Cost Reduction</div>'
            f'<div style="font-size:1.6em;color:#00C4B4;">{savings:.1f}%</div></div></div>'
        )
        if "breakdown" in cost_data:
            rows = "".join(f"<tr><td>{_esc(k)}</td><td>${v:,.4f}</td></tr>"
                           for k, v in cost_data["breakdown"].items())
            body += (f'<table style="margin-top:16px;"><thead><tr><th>Item</th>'
                     f"<th>Cost</th></tr></thead><tbody>{rows}</tbody></table>")
        self._sections.append({"heading": "Cost Analysis", "body": body})
        return self

    def add_model_provenance(self, models: list[dict]) -> ReportBuilder:
        if not models:
            return self
        rows = ""
        for m in models:
            rows += (
                f"<tr><td>{_badge(m.get('name', ''))}</td>"
                f"<td>{_esc(m.get('version', ''))}</td>"
                f"<td>{_esc(m.get('provider', ''))}</td>"
                f"<td>{_esc(m.get('purpose', ''))}</td>"
                f"<td><code>{_esc(m.get('endpoint', 'local'))}</code></td></tr>"
            )
        body = (
            "<table><thead><tr><th>Model</th><th>Version</th><th>Provider</th>"
            f"<th>Purpose</th><th>Endpoint</th></tr></thead><tbody>{rows}</tbody></table>"
        )
        self._sections.append({"heading": "Model Provenance", "body": body})
        return self

    # -- Rendering ---------------------------------------------------------

    def render_html(self) -> str:
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC")
        sects = "".join(
            f'<section><h2>{_esc(s["heading"])}</h2>{s["body"]}</section>'
            for s in self._sections
        )
        css = (
            ":root{--bg:#0A0F1E;--sf:#0F1829;--tx:#E2E8F0;--ac:#00C4B4;--mu:#94A3B8;--ra:#1E2D4A}"
            "*{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}"
            "body{font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,sans-serif;background:var(--bg);"
            "color:var(--tx);line-height:1.6;padding:24px;max-width:1100px;margin:0 auto}"
            "header{border-bottom:3px solid var(--ac);padding-bottom:16px;margin-bottom:32px}"
            "header h1{font-size:1.8em;color:var(--ac)}"
            "header .meta{font-size:.85em;color:var(--mu);margin-top:4px}"
            "section{margin-bottom:32px}"
            "h2{color:var(--ac);font-size:1.3em;margin-bottom:12px;"
            "border-left:4px solid var(--ac);padding-left:12px}"
            "table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:.9em}"
            "th{background:var(--sf);color:var(--ac);padding:10px 12px;"
            "text-align:left;border-bottom:2px solid var(--ac)}"
            "td{padding:8px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid #2a2a3e}"
            "tr:nth-child(even){background:var(--ra)}"
            "code{background:#2a2a3e;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:3px;font-size:.9em}"
            "blockquote{border-left:3px solid var(--ac);padding:8px 16px;margin:12px 0;"
            "background:var(--sf);border-radius:0 4px 4px 0;font-style:italic;color:#bbb}"
            "footer{margin-top:40px;padding-top:16px;border-top:1px solid #333;"
            "font-size:.8em;color:var(--mu);text-align:center}"
            "@media print{:root{--bg:#fff;--sf:#f5f5f5;--tx:#222;--mu:#666;--ra:#f9f9f9}"
            "body{padding:0}header h1{color:#222}h2{color:#333;border-left-color:#333}"
            "th{background:#eee;color:#333;border-bottom-color:#333}"
            "code{background:#eee}blockquote{background:#f5f5f5}}"
        )
        return (
            f"<!DOCTYPE html><html lang='en'><head><meta charset='utf-8'>"
            f"<meta name='viewport' content='width=device-width,initial-scale=1'>"
            f"<title>{_esc(self._title)}</title><style>{css}</style></head><body>"
            f"<header><h1>{_esc(self._title)}</h1>"
            f"<div class='meta'>Generated {now} | ResistAI v{_VERSION}</div></header>"
            f"{sects}<footer>ResistAI AMR Drug Discovery Platform | "
            f"BioHack Evolved 2025</footer></body></html>"
        )

    def save(self, output_path: str) -> str:
        """Save report as HTML. Attempt PDF via weasyprint if available."""
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(output_path) or ".", exist_ok=True)
        html_content = self.render_html()
        html_path = output_path.rsplit(".", 1)[0] + ".html"
        with open(html_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(html_content)
        logger.info("HTML report saved: %s", html_path)

        pdf_path = output_path.rsplit(".", 1)[0] + ".pdf"
        try:
            from weasyprint import HTML as WeasyprintHTML  # type: ignore[import-untyped]
            WeasyprintHTML(string=html_content).write_pdf(pdf_path)
            logger.info("PDF report saved: %s", pdf_path)
            return pdf_path
        except ImportError:
            logger.debug("weasyprint not available; skipping PDF generation")
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("PDF generation failed: %s", exc)
        return html_path

    # -- Factory -----------------------------------------------------------

    @classmethod
    def from_pipeline_results(cls, results: Any) -> ReportBuilder:
        """Create a pre-filled report from a PipelineResults object.

        Accepts both ``src.sdk.results.PipelineResults`` and
        ``src.models.schemas.PipelineResults`` (duck-typed).
        """
        b = cls()
        _g = getattr
        name = _g(results, "target_name", "") or _g(results, "pathogen", "")
        steps = _g(results, "steps_executed", [])
        duration = 0.0
        try:
            from datetime import datetime as _dt
            t0 = _dt.fromisoformat(str(_g(results, "started_at", "")))
            t1 = _dt.fromisoformat(str(_g(results, "finished_at", "")))
            duration = (t1 - t0).total_seconds()
        except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
            pass

        step_model = {"fold": "ESMFold", "structure": "ESMFold", "screen": "Boltz-2",
                       "dock": "DiffDock", "generate": "MolMIM", "design": "RFdiffusion"}
        models = list(dict.fromkeys(step_model[s] for s in steps if s in step_model))
        if "design" in steps:
            models.append("ProteinMPNN")
        models = models or (list(steps) if steps else ["pipeline"])

        def _to_dicts(items: list) -> list[dict]:
            return [x.to_dict() if hasattr(x, "to_dict") else x for x in items]

        # Build candidates from screening hits or top_candidates
        top = _g(results, "top_candidates", [])
        hits = _g(results, "screening_hits", [])
        if top:
            cands = _to_dicts(top)
        elif hits:
            cands = [{"rank": i, "drug_name": _g(h, "drug_name", ""),
                       "smiles": _g(h, "smiles", ""), "target_name": name,
                       "binding_affinity": _g(h, "confidence", 0), "safety_score": 0.0,
                       "overall_score": _g(h, "confidence", 0),
                       "regulatory_pathway": "505(b)(2)"} for i, h in enumerate(hits, 1)]
        else:
            cands = []

        targets = _to_dicts(_g(results, "targets", []))
        novel = [m.to_dict() if hasattr(m, "to_dict") else
                 {"smiles": _g(m, "smiles", ""), "score": _g(m, "score", 0),
                  "score_type": _g(m, "score_type", ""), "seed_smiles": _g(m, "seed_smiles", "")}
                 for m in _g(results, "novel_molecules", [])]
        binder = _g(results, "binder_design", None)
        binders = [{"success": _g(binder, "success", False),
                     "sequences": _g(binder, "sequences", [])}] if binder else []
        safety = _to_dicts(_g(results, "safety_reports", []))
        analysis = _g(results, "analysis", None)

        b.add_executive_summary(name, len(targets) or 1, len(cands), models,
                                duration, analysis or "")
        if targets:
            b.add_target_table(targets)
        if cands:
            b.add_candidate_table(cands)
        if novel:
            b.add_novel_molecules(novel)
        if binders:
            b.add_binder_design(binders)
        if safety:
            b.add_safety_profile(safety)
        b.add_regulatory_pathway()
        b.add_model_provenance([{"name": m, "version": "latest",
                                  "provider": "NVIDIA BioNeMo / OSS",
                                  "purpose": "AMR target discovery",
                                  "endpoint": "NIM API"} for m in models])
        return b

--- END OF FILE: .\src\sdk\report_pdf.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\sdk\results.py ---
"""Pipeline results container for the ResistAI SDK.

Collects outputs from each pipeline step into a single, navigable object
with typed accessors and export helpers.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Optional

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass
class ScreeningHit:
    """A single drug-target screening result."""

    drug_name: str = ""
    smiles: str = ""
    confidence: float = 0.0
    ptm_score: float = 0.0
    iptm_score: float = 0.0
    ligand_iptm: float = 0.0
    structure_cif: str = ""
    raw: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass
class DockingPose:
    """A single docking pose from DiffDock."""

    ligand_sdf: str = ""
    confidence: float = 0.0
    drug_name: str = ""
    smiles: str = ""


@dataclass
class NovelMolecule:
    """A molecule generated by MolMIM."""

    smiles: str = ""
    score: float = 0.0
    score_type: str = ""
    seed_smiles: str = ""


@dataclass
class BinderDesign:
    """A protein binder designed via RFdiffusion + ProteinMPNN."""

    backbone_pdb: str = ""
    sequences: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    success: bool = False


@dataclass
class ValidationResult:
    """Binding validation of a novel molecule against the target."""

    smiles: str = ""
    confidence: float = 0.0
    iptm_score: float = 0.0
    success: bool = False


@dataclass
class PipelineResults:
    """Aggregated results from a full pipeline run.

    Each attribute corresponds to one pipeline step and is ``None`` until
    that step has been executed.
    """

    target_name: str = ""
    target_sequence: str = ""
    started_at: str = ""
    finished_at: str = ""
    steps_executed: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    # Step outputs
    structure: Optional[str] = None
    structure_length: int = 0
    fold_elapsed_ms: float = 0.0

    screening_hits: list[ScreeningHit] = field(default_factory=list)

    docking_poses: list[DockingPose] = field(default_factory=list)

    novel_molecules: list[NovelMolecule] = field(default_factory=list)

    validated_molecules: list[ValidationResult] = field(default_factory=list)

    binder_design: Optional[BinderDesign] = None

    analysis: Optional[str] = None

    errors: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Export helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Serialize all results to a plain dictionary."""
        return {
            "target_name": self.target_name,
            "target_sequence_length": len(self.target_sequence),
            "started_at": self.started_at,
            "finished_at": self.finished_at,
            "steps_executed": self.steps_executed,
            "structure_available": self.structure is not None,
            "structure_length": self.structure_length,
            "fold_elapsed_ms": self.fold_elapsed_ms,
            "screening_hits": [
                {"drug": h.drug_name, "smiles": h.smiles, "confidence": h.confidence,
                 "ptm": h.ptm_score, "iptm": h.iptm_score}
                for h in self.screening_hits
            ],
            "docking_poses": [
                {"drug": p.drug_name, "smiles": p.smiles, "confidence": p.confidence}
                for p in self.docking_poses
            ],
            "novel_molecules": [
                {"smiles": m.smiles, "score": m.score, "type": m.score_type}
                for m in self.novel_molecules
            ],
            "validated_molecules": [
                {"smiles": v.smiles, "confidence": v.confidence, "iptm": v.iptm_score}
                for v in self.validated_molecules
            ],
            "binder_design": {
                "success": self.binder_design.success,
                "num_sequences": len(self.binder_design.sequences),
            } if self.binder_design else None,
            "analysis": self.analysis,
            "errors": self.errors,
        }

    def to_json(self, indent: int = 2) -> str:
        """Serialize results as a JSON string."""
        return json.dumps(self.to_dict(), indent=indent, default=str)

    def to_report(self) -> str:
        """Generate a human-readable Markdown report."""
        lines: list[str] = []
        lines.append(f"# ResistAI Pipeline Report: {self.target_name}")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(f"**Sequence length:** {len(self.target_sequence)} aa")
        lines.append(f"**Steps executed:** {', '.join(self.steps_executed)}")
        lines.append(f"**Started:** {self.started_at}")
        lines.append(f"**Finished:** {self.finished_at}")
        lines.append("")

        if self.structure is not None:
            lines.append("## Structure Prediction (ESMFold)")
            lines.append(f"- PDB length: {self.structure_length} characters")
            lines.append(f"- Elapsed: {self.fold_elapsed_ms:.0f} ms")
            lines.append("")

        if self.screening_hits:
            lines.append("## Drug Screening (Boltz-2)")
            lines.append(f"- Hits: {len(self.screening_hits)}")
            lines.append("")
            lines.append("| Rank | Drug | Confidence | iPTM |")
            lines.append("|------|------|-----------|------|")
            for i, h in enumerate(self.screening_hits[:20], 1):
                lines.append(
                    f"| {i} | {h.drug_name} | {h.confidence:.3f} | {h.iptm_score:.3f} |"
                )
            lines.append("")

        if self.docking_poses:
            lines.append("## Molecular Docking (DiffDock)")
            lines.append(f"- Poses generated: {len(self.docking_poses)}")
            lines.append("")
            for i, p in enumerate(self.docking_poses[:10], 1):
                lines.append(f"{i}. **{p.drug_name}** (confidence {p.confidence:.3f})")
            lines.append("")

        if self.novel_molecules:
            lines.append("## Novel Molecules (MolMIM)")
            lines.append(f"- Generated: {len(self.novel_molecules)}")
            lines.append("")
            lines.append("| # | SMILES | Score | Type |")
            lines.append("|---|--------|-------|------|")
            for i, m in enumerate(self.novel_molecules[:20], 1):
                smi = m.smiles[:60] + ("..." if len(m.smiles) > 60 else "")
                lines.append(f"| {i} | `{smi}` | {m.score:.3f} | {m.score_type} |")
            lines.append("")

        if self.validated_molecules:
            lines.append("## Validation (Boltz-2)")
            lines.append(f"- Validated: {len(self.validated_molecules)}")
            lines.append("")
            for i, v in enumerate(self.validated_molecules[:10], 1):
                smi = v.smiles[:50] + ("..." if len(v.smiles) > 50 else "")
                lines.append(f"{i}. `{smi}` -- confidence {v.confidence:.3f}")
            lines.append("")

        if self.binder_design:
            lines.append("## Binder Design (RFdiffusion + ProteinMPNN)")
            lines.append(f"- Success: {self.binder_design.success}")
            lines.append(f"- Designed sequences: {len(self.binder_design.sequences)}")
            lines.append("")

        if self.analysis:
            lines.append("## AI Analysis")
            lines.append(self.analysis)
            lines.append("")

        if self.errors:
            lines.append("## Errors")
            for err in self.errors:
                lines.append(f"- **{err.get('step', '?')}**: {err.get('error', '?')}")
            lines.append("")

        return "\n".join(lines)

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        parts = [f"PipelineResults(target={self.target_name!r}"]
        parts.append(f"steps={self.steps_executed}")
        if self.screening_hits:
            parts.append(f"hits={len(self.screening_hits)}")
        if self.novel_molecules:
            parts.append(f"novel={len(self.novel_molecules)}")
        return ", ".join(parts) + ")"

--- END OF FILE: .\src\sdk\results.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\sdk\target.py ---
"""Target protein representation for the ResistAI Pipeline SDK.

Supports construction from raw amino acid sequences, UniProt accessions,
or PDB identifiers.  Fetching from remote databases is done synchronously
via httpx so the SDK stays simple to use in scripts and notebooks.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional

import httpx

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_UNIPROT_BASE = "https://rest.uniprot.org/uniprotkb"
_PDB_FASTA_BASE = "https://www.rcsb.org/fasta/entry"
_PDB_FILE_BASE = "https://files.rcsb.org/download"
_TIMEOUT = 30.0


def _parse_fasta(text: str) -> str:
    """Extract the first sequence from a FASTA string."""
    lines: list[str] = []
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if line.startswith(">"):
            if lines:
                break  # stop at second header
            continue
        lines.append(line.strip())
    return "".join(lines)


@dataclass
class Target:
    """A protein target for the drug-discovery pipeline.

    Attributes:
        sequence:   Amino acid sequence (single-letter codes).
        name:       Human-readable label (e.g. ``"NDM-1"``).
        source:     How the target was created (``"sequence"``, ``"uniprot"``, ``"pdb"``).
        source_id:  UniProt accession or PDB ID when applicable.
        pdb_string: Raw PDB file contents, populated by ``from_pdb`` or after folding.
        metadata:   Arbitrary extra data attached to this target.
    """

    sequence: str = ""
    name: str = ""
    source: str = "sequence"
    source_id: str = ""
    pdb_string: str = ""
    metadata: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Constructors
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @classmethod
    def from_sequence(cls, sequence: str, *, name: str = "") -> Target:
        """Create a target directly from an amino acid sequence.

        Args:
            sequence: Amino acid string (whitespace is stripped).
            name:     Optional label for this target.
        """
        cleaned = "".join(sequence.split()).upper()
        if not cleaned:
            raise ValueError("sequence must not be empty")
        return cls(
            sequence=cleaned,
            name=name or f"seq_{len(cleaned)}aa",
            source="sequence",
        )

    @classmethod
    def from_uniprot(cls, accession: str, *, name: str = "") -> Target:
        """Fetch a protein sequence from the UniProt REST API.

        Args:
            accession: UniProt accession (e.g. ``"C7C422"``).
            name:      Optional label; defaults to the accession.

        Raises:
            RuntimeError: If the fetch fails after retries.
        """
        accession = accession.strip().upper()
        url = f"{_UNIPROT_BASE}/{accession}.fasta"
        try:
            resp = httpx.get(url, timeout=_TIMEOUT, follow_redirects=True)
            resp.raise_for_status()
        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Failed to fetch UniProt {accession}: {exc}"
            ) from exc

        seq = _parse_fasta(resp.text)
        if not seq:
            raise RuntimeError(f"No sequence found in UniProt response for {accession}")

        label = name or accession
        logger.info("Fetched %s from UniProt (%d aa)", label, len(seq))
        return cls(
            sequence=seq,
            name=label,
            source="uniprot",
            source_id=accession,
            metadata={"fasta_header": resp.text.split("\n", 1)[0]},
        )

    @classmethod
    def from_pdb(cls, pdb_id: str, *, name: str = "") -> Target:
        """Fetch a protein from the RCSB PDB (sequence + structure).

        Downloads both the FASTA sequence and the PDB coordinate file.

        Args:
            pdb_id: Four-character PDB identifier (e.g. ``"3SPU"``).
            name:   Optional label; defaults to the PDB ID.

        Raises:
            RuntimeError: If the fetch fails.
        """
        pdb_id = pdb_id.strip().upper()

        # Fetch FASTA for the sequence
        fasta_url = f"{_PDB_FASTA_BASE}/{pdb_id}"
        try:
            fasta_resp = httpx.get(fasta_url, timeout=_TIMEOUT, follow_redirects=True)
            fasta_resp.raise_for_status()
        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to fetch PDB FASTA {pdb_id}: {exc}") from exc

        seq = _parse_fasta(fasta_resp.text)
        if not seq:
            raise RuntimeError(f"No sequence found in PDB FASTA for {pdb_id}")

        # Fetch PDB coordinate file
        pdb_url = f"{_PDB_FILE_BASE}/{pdb_id}.pdb"
        pdb_string = ""
        try:
            pdb_resp = httpx.get(pdb_url, timeout=_TIMEOUT, follow_redirects=True)
            pdb_resp.raise_for_status()
            pdb_string = pdb_resp.text
        except httpx.HTTPError:
            logger.warning("Could not fetch PDB file for %s; structure unavailable", pdb_id)

        label = name or pdb_id
        logger.info("Fetched %s from PDB (%d aa, structure=%s)", label, len(seq), bool(pdb_string))
        return cls(
            sequence=seq,
            name=label,
            source="pdb",
            source_id=pdb_id,
            pdb_string=pdb_string,
            metadata={"fasta_header": fasta_resp.text.split("\n", 1)[0]},
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Utilities
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @property
    def has_structure(self) -> bool:
        """Whether a 3D structure (PDB string) is available."""
        return bool(self.pdb_string and len(self.pdb_string) > 100)

    @property
    def length(self) -> int:
        """Number of amino acids in the sequence."""
        return len(self.sequence)

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        struct = "with structure" if self.has_structure else "no structure"
        return f"Target(name={self.name!r}, {self.length}aa, {struct}, source={self.source})"

--- END OF FILE: .\src\sdk\target.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\sdk\tracker.py ---
"""Experiment tracking for ResistAI pipeline runs.

SQLite-backed logger that records every pipeline run, prediction,
and molecule candidate for building proprietary datasets over time.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import json
import os
import sqlite3
import time
import uuid
from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Generator

_DEFAULT_DB = ".resistai/experiments.db"

_SCHEMA = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS experiments (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    pathogen TEXT DEFAULT '',
    status TEXT DEFAULT 'running',
    duration_ms REAL DEFAULT 0,
    models_used TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
    metadata TEXT DEFAULT '{}',
    created_at REAL NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS predictions (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    experiment_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    model TEXT NOT NULL,
    input_type TEXT DEFAULT '',
    input_data TEXT DEFAULT '{}',
    output_data TEXT DEFAULT '{}',
    score REAL DEFAULT 0,
    elapsed_ms REAL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at REAL NOT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (experiment_id) REFERENCES experiments(id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS molecules (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    experiment_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    smiles TEXT NOT NULL,
    source TEXT DEFAULT 'generated',
    qed_score REAL DEFAULT 0,
    binding_score REAL DEFAULT 0,
    lipinski_pass INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    parent_smiles TEXT DEFAULT '',
    generation INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at REAL NOT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (experiment_id) REFERENCES experiments(id)
);
"""


class ExperimentTracker:
    """SQLite-backed experiment tracker."""

    def __init__(self, db_path: str = _DEFAULT_DB) -> None:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(db_path) or ".", exist_ok=True)
        self._db_path = db_path
        self._conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, check_same_thread=False)
        self._conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self._conn.executescript(_SCHEMA)

    def start_experiment(
        self, name: str, pathogen: str = "", metadata: dict | None = None
    ) -> str:
        eid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
        self._conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO experiments (id, name, pathogen, metadata, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)",
            (eid, name, pathogen, json.dumps(metadata or {}), time.time()),
        )
        self._conn.commit()
        return eid

    def log_prediction(
        self,
        experiment_id: str,
        model: str,
        input_data: dict,
        output_data: dict,
        score: float = 0.0,
        elapsed_ms: float = 0.0,
        input_type: str = "",
    ) -> None:
        self._conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO predictions (experiment_id, model, input_type, input_data, output_data, score, elapsed_ms, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (experiment_id, model, input_type, json.dumps(input_data), json.dumps(output_data), score, elapsed_ms, time.time()),
        )
        self._conn.commit()

    def log_molecule(
        self,
        experiment_id: str,
        smiles: str,
        source: str = "generated",
        qed_score: float = 0.0,
        binding_score: float = 0.0,
        lipinski_pass: bool = False,
        parent_smiles: str = "",
        generation: int = 0,
    ) -> None:
        self._conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO molecules (experiment_id, smiles, source, qed_score, binding_score, lipinski_pass, parent_smiles, generation, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (experiment_id, smiles, source, qed_score, binding_score, int(lipinski_pass), parent_smiles, generation, time.time()),
        )
        self._conn.commit()

    def end_experiment(
        self,
        experiment_id: str,
        status: str = "completed",
        duration_ms: float = 0.0,
        models_used: list[str] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._conn.execute(
            "UPDATE experiments SET status=?, duration_ms=?, models_used=? WHERE id=?",
            (status, duration_ms, json.dumps(models_used or []), experiment_id),
        )
        self._conn.commit()

    def get_experiment(self, experiment_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self._conn.execute("SELECT * FROM experiments WHERE id=?", (experiment_id,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            return {}
        exp = dict(row)
        exp["models_used"] = json.loads(exp.get("models_used", "[]"))
        exp["metadata"] = json.loads(exp.get("metadata", "{}"))
        exp["predictions"] = [
            {**dict(r), "input_data": json.loads(r["input_data"]), "output_data": json.loads(r["output_data"])}
            for r in self._conn.execute("SELECT * FROM predictions WHERE experiment_id=? ORDER BY created_at", (experiment_id,))
        ]
        exp["molecules"] = [
            dict(r) for r in self._conn.execute("SELECT * FROM molecules WHERE experiment_id=? ORDER BY binding_score DESC", (experiment_id,))
        ]
        return exp

    def list_experiments(self, limit: int = 50) -> list[dict]:
        rows = self._conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, name, pathogen, status, duration_ms, models_used, created_at FROM experiments ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?",
            (limit,),
        ).fetchall()
        results = []
        for r in rows:
            d = dict(r)
            d["models_used"] = json.loads(d.get("models_used", "[]"))
            results.append(d)
        return results

    def get_stats(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        exp_count = self._conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM experiments").fetchone()[0]
        pred_count = self._conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM predictions").fetchone()[0]
        mol_count = self._conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM molecules").fetchone()[0]
        avg_binding = self._conn.execute("SELECT AVG(binding_score) FROM molecules WHERE binding_score > 0").fetchone()[0] or 0
        avg_qed = self._conn.execute("SELECT AVG(qed_score) FROM molecules WHERE qed_score > 0").fetchone()[0] or 0
        top_models = self._conn.execute(
            "SELECT model, COUNT(*) as cnt FROM predictions GROUP BY model ORDER BY cnt DESC LIMIT 5"
        ).fetchall()
        return {
            "total_experiments": exp_count,
            "total_predictions": pred_count,
            "total_molecules": mol_count,
            "avg_binding_score": round(avg_binding, 4),
            "avg_qed_score": round(avg_qed, 4),
            "top_models": [{"model": r[0], "count": r[1]} for r in top_models],
        }

    def export_molecules_csv(self, output_path: str) -> int:
        rows = self._conn.execute(
            "SELECT m.smiles, m.source, m.qed_score, m.binding_score, m.lipinski_pass, "
            "m.parent_smiles, m.generation, e.pathogen, e.name as experiment "
            "FROM molecules m JOIN experiments e ON m.experiment_id = e.id "
            "ORDER BY m.binding_score DESC"
        ).fetchall()
        with open(output_path, "w", newline="") as f:
            writer = csv.writer(f)
            writer.writerow(["smiles", "source", "qed_score", "binding_score", "lipinski_pass", "parent_smiles", "generation", "pathogen", "experiment"])
            for r in rows:
                writer.writerow(list(r))
        return len(rows)

    @contextmanager
    def experiment(self, name: str, pathogen: str = "", metadata: dict | None = None) -> Generator[_ExperimentContext, None, None]:
        eid = self.start_experiment(name, pathogen, metadata)
        ctx = _ExperimentContext(self, eid)
        t0 = time.time()
        try:
            yield ctx
            elapsed = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
            self.end_experiment(eid, "completed", elapsed, ctx.models_used)
        except Exception:
            elapsed = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
            self.end_experiment(eid, "failed", elapsed, ctx.models_used)
            raise

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._conn.close()


class _ExperimentContext:
    """Context object yielded by ExperimentTracker.experiment()."""

    def __init__(self, tracker: ExperimentTracker, experiment_id: str) -> None:
        self._tracker = tracker
        self.experiment_id = experiment_id
        self.models_used: list[str] = []

    def log_prediction(self, model: str, input_data: dict, output_data: dict,
                       score: float = 0.0, elapsed_ms: float = 0.0) -> None:
        if model not in self.models_used:
            self.models_used.append(model)
        self._tracker.log_prediction(self.experiment_id, model, input_data, output_data, score, elapsed_ms)

    def log_molecule(self, smiles: str, source: str = "generated", qed_score: float = 0.0,
                     binding_score: float = 0.0, lipinski_pass: bool = False,
                     parent_smiles: str = "", generation: int = 0) -> None:
        self._tracker.log_molecule(self.experiment_id, smiles, source, qed_score, binding_score, lipinski_pass, parent_smiles, generation)

--- END OF FILE: .\src\sdk\tracker.py ---

--- START OF FILE: .\src\sdk\webhooks.py ---
"""Webhook / notification system for ResistAI pipeline events.

Enables integration with external services (Slack, email, CI/CD)
when pipeline runs complete or other significant events occur.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import logging
import threading
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Callable

import requests

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

EVENTS = [
    "pipeline.started",
    "pipeline.completed",
    "pipeline.failed",
    "molecule.generated",
    "binder.designed",
    "experiment.completed",
]


@dataclass
class WebhookConfig:
    url: str
    events: list[str] = field(default_factory=lambda: ["pipeline.completed"])
    headers: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    secret: str = ""
    active: bool = True


class WebhookManager:
    """Manages webhook registrations and dispatches events."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._hooks: list[WebhookConfig] = []
        self._history: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    # -- registration --------------------------------------------------------

    def register(
        self,
        url: str,
        events: list[str] | None = None,
        headers: dict[str, str] | None = None,
        secret: str = "",
    ) -> WebhookConfig:
        """Register a new webhook endpoint."""
        resolved_events = events or ["pipeline.completed"]
        invalid = [e for e in resolved_events if e not in EVENTS]
        if invalid:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown event(s): {invalid}")
        hook = WebhookConfig(
            url=url,
            events=resolved_events,
            headers=headers or {},
            secret=secret,
        )
        with self._lock:
            self._hooks.append(hook)
        logger.info("Registered webhook %s for %s", url, resolved_events)
        return hook

    def unregister(self, url: str) -> bool:
        """Remove a webhook by URL. Returns True if found."""
        with self._lock:
            before = len(self._hooks)
            self._hooks = [h for h in self._hooks if h.url != url]
            removed = len(self._hooks) < before
        if removed:
            logger.info("Unregistered webhook %s", url)
        return removed

    # -- dispatch ------------------------------------------------------------

    def dispatch(self, event: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Send *event* to every matching active webhook (blocking)."""
        if event not in EVENTS:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown event: {event}")
        with self._lock:
            targets = [h for h in self._hooks if h.active and event in h.events]
        results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for hook in targets:
            results.append(self._deliver(hook, event, payload))
        return results

    def dispatch_async(self, event: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Fire-and-forget dispatch in a background thread."""
        t = threading.Thread(
            target=self.dispatch, args=(event, payload), daemon=True
        )
        t.start()

    # -- delivery ------------------------------------------------------------

    def _deliver(
        self, hook: WebhookConfig, event: str, payload: dict[str, Any]
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        body = json.dumps(
            {"event": event, "timestamp": time.time(), "data": payload},
            default=str,
        )
        headers = {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "X-ResistAI-Event": event,
            **hook.headers,
        }
        if hook.secret:
            headers["X-ResistAI-Signature"] = self._sign_payload(body, hook.secret)

        result: dict[str, Any] = {"url": hook.url, "event": event}
        start = time.monotonic()
        try:
            resp = requests.post(hook.url, data=body, headers=headers, timeout=10)
            result["status"] = resp.status_code
            result["ok"] = 200 <= resp.status_code < 300
        except requests.RequestException as exc:
            result["status"] = 0
            result["ok"] = False
            result["error"] = str(exc)
            logger.warning("Webhook delivery failed for %s: %s", hook.url, exc)
        result["duration_ms"] = round((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000, 1)

        with self._lock:
            self._history.append(result)
            if len(self._history) > 500:
                self._history = self._history[-500:]
        return result

    @staticmethod
    def _sign_payload(payload: str, secret: str) -> str:
        """HMAC-SHA256 hex signature."""
        return hmac.new(
            secret.encode(), payload.encode(), hashlib.sha256
        ).hexdigest()

    # -- introspection -------------------------------------------------------

    def get_history(self, limit: int = 50) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Return recent delivery history (newest first)."""
        with self._lock:
            return list(reversed(self._history[-limit:]))

    def list_hooks(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Return serialisable list of registered webhooks."""
        with self._lock:
            return [
                {
                    "url": h.url,
                    "events": h.events,
                    "active": h.active,
                    "has_secret": bool(h.secret),
                }
                for h in self._hooks
            ]


# -- Slack helpers -----------------------------------------------------------

def slack_webhook(webhook_url: str) -> WebhookConfig:
    """Pre-configured :class:`WebhookConfig` for Slack incoming webhooks."""
    return WebhookConfig(
        url=webhook_url,
        events=["pipeline.completed", "pipeline.failed"],
        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
    )


def format_slack_message(event: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Format a pipeline event as a Slack Block Kit message."""
    color = "#2eb886" if "completed" in event else "#dc3545"
    header = f"ResistAI | {event}"
    fields = [
        {"type": "mrkdwn", "text": f"*{k}:*\n{v}"}
        for k, v in list(payload.items())[:8]
    ]
    return {
        "attachments": [
            {
                "color": color,
                "blocks": [
                    {
                        "type": "header",
                        "text": {"type": "plain_text", "text": header},
                    },
                    {"type": "section", "fields": fields} if fields else None,
                ],
            }
        ]
    }
